> DEVELOPER

Implement the following plan:

# Issue #10: Suffix-Checkboxen (/m /p /am /mm) statt Freitext im Rufzeichen

## Context
Operatoren geben aktuell Suffixe wie `/m` (mobil), `/p` (portabel) direkt im Rufzeichen-Feld ein (z.B. "OE8YML/m"). Das ist fehleranfällig und uneinheitlich. Stattdessen sollen Checkboxen/Toggles für `/m`, `/p`, `/am`, `/mm` neben dem Rufzeichen-Feld erscheinen. Der Suffix wird separat gespeichert und in den Auswertungen mitangezeigt.

## Bestandsanalyse
- `exercise_attendance` hat bereits `callsign_suffix TEXT` — **unbenutzt**
- `signal_reports` hat **keinen** Suffix-Spalte
- Suffix ist pro Report sinnvoll (Operator kann Status wechseln)
- Auswertungen aggregieren Teilnehmer per `operator_id`, zeigen `callsign` an

## Änderungen

### 1. Schema-Migration: `server/src/db/schema.ts`
- Neue Spalte `suffix TEXT DEFAULT NULL` in `signal_reports` (ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN, nicht-destruktiv)
- Gültige Werte: `'/m'`, `'/p'`, `'/am'`, `'/mm'`, `NULL`

### 2. API: `server/src/routes/exercises.ts`
- **POST** `/exercises/:id/reports`: `suffix` Feld akzeptieren und speichern
- **PATCH** `/exercises/:id/reports/:rid`: `suffix` Feld akzeptieren
- **GET** `/exercises/:id/reports`: `suffix` im SELECT mit zurückgeben
- **GET** `/exercises/:id/stats` (Participants-Query): Suffix mit aggregieren — `GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT sr.suffix)` als `suffixes` oder einfach den häufigsten/letzten Suffix pro Operator

### 3. API: `server/src/routes/reports.ts`
- Stats-Participants-Query: Ebenfalls Suffix mitliefern (gleicher Pattern wie exercises stats)

### 4. Client Types: `client/src/types/stats.ts`
- `Participant` Interface: `suffix?: string | null` hinzufügen

### 5. UI Eingabe: `client/src/components/LandMode.tsx` + `BundMode.tsx`
- `EntryForm` Interface: `suffix: string` ('' | '/m' | '/p' | '/am' | '/mm')
- **Toggle-Buttons** neben dem Rufzeichen-Feld (4 kleine Buttons: m, p, am, mm)
  - Nur einer aktiv (Radio-Verhalten), nochmal klicken = deselect
  - Visuell: grauer Button, bei aktiv blauer Hintergrund
  - Kompakt: inline neben CallsignInput
- `suffix` wird im POST/PATCH mitgesendet
- In der Report-Anzeige (jede Zeile): Callsign + Suffix anzeigen (z.B. "OE8YML/m")

### 6. Auswertung Web: `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` + `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`
- Teilnehmer-Tabelle: Suffix neben Rufzeichen anzeigen
- Falls ein Operator mehrere Suffixe hat (unwahrscheinlich): alle zeigen

### 7. Auswertung PDF: `server/src/services/pdf.ts`
- `Participant` Interface: `suffix` Feld hinzufügen
- `renderParticipants()`: Callsign + Suffix anzeigen

### 8. CHANGELOG: `CHANGELOG-2026-02-24.md`
- Issue #10 Eintrag

## Dateien

| Datei | Änderung |
|-------|----------|
| `server/src/db/schema.ts` | ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN `suffix` |
| `server/src/routes/exercises.ts` | POST/PATCH/GET reports: suffix Feld |
| `server/src/routes/reports.ts` | Stats participants: suffix mitliefern |
| `server/src/routes/pdf.ts` | Suffix in PDF-Stats Query |
| `client/src/types/stats.ts` | Participant.suffix |
| `client/src/components/LandMode.tsx` | Suffix-Toggles + form submit |
| `client/src/components/BundMode.tsx` | Suffix-Toggles + form submit |
| `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` | Suffix in Teilnehmer-Tabelle |
| `client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx` | Suffix in Teilnehmer-Tabelle |
| `server/src/services/pdf.ts` | Suffix in PDF Teilnehmer |
| `CHANGELOG-2026-02-24.md` | Dokumentation |

## Verifikation
1. `nix-shell -p nodejs --run "npm run build"` kompiliert sauber
2. Neuer Report: Suffix-Toggles sichtbar, /m auswählen → Report zeigt "OE8YML/m"
3. Auswertung (Einzel-Übung): Teilnehmer-Liste zeigt Suffix
4. Auswertung (Aggregiert): Teilnehmer-Liste zeigt Suffix
5. PDF-Download: Teilnehmer mit Suffix
6. Bestehende Reports ohne Suffix: funktionieren weiterhin (NULL = kein Suffix)
7. Deploy mit `./deploy-production.sh`

     1→import { Router } from 'express';
     2→import { randomUUID } from 'crypto';
     3→import { getDb } from '../db/database.js';
     4→import { AuthRequest, requireRole } from '../middleware/auth.js';
     5→
     6→export const exercisesRouter = Router();
     7→
     8→// List all exercises with summary stats
     9→exercisesRouter.get('/', (_req, res) => {
    10→  const db = getDb();
    11→  const exercises = db.prepare(`
    12→    SELECT e.id, e.date, e.name, e.notes, e.oe_link_enabled, e.exercise_type, e.simplex_bezirke, e.simplex_bl_codes,
    13→      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0) as participant_count,
    14→      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL) as report_count
    15→    FROM exercises e ORDER BY e.date ASC
    16→  `).all();
    17→  res.json(exercises);
    18→});
    19→
    20→// Create exercise (admin only)
    21→exercisesRouter.post('/', requireRole('admin'), (req, res) => {
    22→  const { date, name, oe_link_enabled, exercise_type, simplex_bezirke, simplex_bl_codes } = req.body;
    23→  if (!date || !name) {
    24→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Datum und Name erforderlich' });
    25→    return;
    26→  }
    27→  const db = getDb();
    28→  const id = randomUUID();
    29→  db.prepare('INSERT INTO exercises (id, date, name, oe_link_enabled, exercise_type, simplex_bezirke, simplex_bl_codes) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)').run(id, date, name, oe_link_enabled !== undefined ? (oe_link_enabled ? 1 : 0) : 1, exercise_type || 'Krisenkommunikationsübung', simplex_bezirke ? 1 : 0, simplex_bl_codes || null);
    30→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(id);
    31→  res.status(201).json(exercise);
    32→});
    33→
    34→// Get full exercise with reports
    35→exercisesRouter.get('/:id', (req, res) => {
    36→  const db = getDb();
    37→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
    38→  if (!exercise) {
    39→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Übung nicht gefunden' });
    40→    return;
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  const repeaters = db.prepare(`
    44→    SELECT er.*, r.short_name, r.site_name, r.band, r.callsign as repeater_callsign,
    45→      r.frequency_mhz, r.offset_mhz, r.ctcss_hz, r.burst_hz, r.type, r.is_linked,
    46→      r.bundesland_code, bl.name as bundesland_name
    47→    FROM exercise_repeaters er
    48→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = er.repeater_id
    49→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = r.bundesland_code
    50→    WHERE er.exercise_id = ?
    51→    ORDER BY r.bundesland_code, r.sort_order
    52→  `).all(req.params.id);
    53→
    54→  const reports = db.prepare(`
    55→    SELECT sr.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, o.qth as operator_qth, sr.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
    56→      r.short_name as repeater_name, r.bundesland_code as repeater_bundesland_code, r.is_linked as repeater_is_linked,
    57→      ep.abbreviation as einstiegspunkt_abbr, ep.site_name as einstiegspunkt_name
    58→    FROM signal_reports sr
    59→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    60→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    61→    LEFT JOIN einstiegspunkte ep ON ep.id = sr.einstiegspunkt_id
    62→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ?
    63→    ORDER BY r.bundesland_code, o.callsign
    64→  `).all(req.params.id);
    65→
    66→  const attendance = db.prepare(`
    67→    SELECT ea.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code
    68→    FROM exercise_attendance ea
    69→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = ea.operator_id
    70→    WHERE ea.exercise_id = ?
    71→    ORDER BY o.callsign
    72→  `).all(req.params.id);
    73→
    74→  res.json({ ...exercise, repeaters, reports, attendance });
    75→});
    76→
    77→// Update exercise notes/name (admin only)
    78→exercisesRouter.patch('/:id', requireRole('admin'), (req, res) => {
    79→  const db = getDb();
    80→  const { notes, name, oe_link_enabled, organisator, exercise_type, simplex_bezirke, simplex_bl_codes } = req.body;
    81→  const updates: string[] = [];
    82→  const params: any[] = [];
    83→  if (notes !== undefined) { updates.push('notes = ?'); params.push(notes); }
    84→  if (name !== undefined) { updates.push('name = ?'); params.push(name); }
    85→  if (oe_link_enabled !== undefined) { updates.push('oe_link_enabled = ?'); params.push(oe_link_enabled ? 1 : 0); }
    86→  if (organisator !== undefined) { updates.push('organisator = ?'); params.push(organisator || null); }
    87→  if (exercise_type !== undefined) { updates.push('exercise_type = ?'); params.push(exercise_type); }
    88→  if (simplex_bezirke !== undefined) { updates.push('simplex_bezirke = ?'); params.push(simplex_bezirke ? 1 : 0); }
    89→  if (simplex_bl_codes !== undefined) { updates.push('simplex_bl_codes = ?'); params.push(simplex_bl_codes || null); }
    90→  if (updates.length === 0) {
    91→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Keine Änderungen' });
    92→    return;
    93→  }
    94→  params.push(req.params.id);
    95→  db.prepare(`UPDATE exercises SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ?`).run(...params);
    96→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
    97→  res.json(exercise);
    98→});

   100→// Delete exercise (admin only)
   101→exercisesRouter.delete('/:id', requireRole('admin'), (req, res) => {
   102→  const db = getDb();
   103→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT id, name, date FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
   104→  if (!exercise) {
   105→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Übung nicht gefunden' });
   106→    return;
   107→  }
   108→  const deleteAll = db.transaction(() => {
   109→    db.prepare('DELETE FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = ?').run(req.params.id);
   110→    db.prepare('DELETE FROM exercise_attendance WHERE exercise_id = ?').run(req.params.id);
   111→    db.prepare('DELETE FROM exercise_repeaters WHERE exercise_id = ?').run(req.params.id);
   112→    db.prepare('DELETE FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').run(req.params.id);
   113→  });
   114→  deleteAll();
   115→  res.json({ success: true });
   116→});
   117→
   118→// Get exercise stats
   119→exercisesRouter.get('/:id/stats', (req, res) => {
   120→  const db = getDb();
   121→  const eid = req.params.id;
   122→  const totalParticipants = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0').get(eid) as any)?.c || 0;
   123→  const totalReports = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL').get(eid) as any)?.c || 0;
   124→  const perRepeater = db.prepare(`
   125→    SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count
   126→    FROM signal_reports sr
   127→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
   128→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0 AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL
   129→    GROUP BY sr.repeater_id
   130→    ORDER BY r.sort_order
   131→  `).all(eid);
   132→
   133→  // Bezirk breakdown: participants + reports per bezirk, grouped by bundesland
   134→  const bezirkStats = db.prepare(`
   135→    SELECT bl.name as bundesland, bl.code as bundesland_code, bl.sort_order as bl_sort,
   136→      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
   137→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
   138→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
   139→    FROM signal_reports sr
   140→    JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code
   141→    JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code
   142→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   143→    GROUP BY bz.code
   144→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code
   145→  `).all(eid);
   146→
   147→  // Bundesland breakdown
   148→  const blStats = db.prepare(`
   149→    SELECT
   150→      COALESCE(bl.name, 'Sonstige') as bundesland,
   151→      o.bundesland_code,
   152→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
   153→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
   154→    FROM signal_reports sr
   155→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   156→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
   157→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   158→    GROUP BY o.bundesland_code
   159→    ORDER BY o.bundesland_code
   160→  `).all(eid);
   161→
   162→  // Participant list: all operators with their report counts
   163→  const participants = db.prepare(`
   164→    SELECT o.callsign, o.name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
   165→      bl.name as bundesland_name,
   166→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count
   167→    FROM signal_reports sr
   168→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   169→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
   170→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   171→    GROUP BY sr.operator_id
   172→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign
   173→  `).all(eid);
   174→
   175→  res.json({ totalParticipants, totalReports, perRepeater, bezirkStats, blStats, participants });
   176→});
   177→
   178→// Exercise repeaters management
   179→exercisesRouter.get('/:id/repeaters', (req, res) => {

   224→// Signal Reports CRUD
   225→exercisesRouter.get('/:id/reports', (req, res) => {
   226→  const db = getDb();

   228→    SELECT sr.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, o.qth as operator_qth, sr.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
   229→      r.short_name as repeater_name, r.bundesland_code as repeater_bundesland_code, r.is_linked as repeater_is_linked,
   230→      ep.abbreviation as einstiegspunkt_abbr, ep.site_name as einstiegspunkt_name

   241→exercisesRouter.post('/:id/reports', (req, res) => {
   242→  const db = getDb();
   243→  const admin = (req as AuthRequest).admin!;
   244→  const { operator_id, repeater_id, readability, strength, db_over_s9, einstiegspunkt_id, is_op_marker, notes, bezirk_code } = req.body;
   245→  console.log(`[report] POST exercise=${req.params.id} op=${operator_id} rep=${repeater_id} r=${readability} s=${strength} bz=${bezirk_code || '?'} by=${admin.username}`);
   246→
   247→  if (!operator_id || !repeater_id) {
   248→    console.log('[report] REJECTED: missing operator_id or repeater_id');
   249→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'operator_id und repeater_id erforderlich' });
   250→    return;
   251→  }
   252→
   253→  try {
   254→    const insertReportAndAttendance = db.transaction(() => {
   255→      const result = db.prepare(`
   256→        INSERT INTO signal_reports (exercise_id, operator_id, repeater_id, readability, strength, db_over_s9, einstiegspunkt_id, is_op_marker, notes, entered_by, bezirk_code)
   257→        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
   258→      `).run(req.params.id, operator_id, repeater_id, readability || null, strength || null, db_over_s9 || null, einstiegspunkt_id || null, is_op_marker ? 1 : 0, notes || null, admin.username, bezirk_code || null);
   259→
   260→      db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO exercise_attendance (exercise_id, operator_id, entered_by) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')
   261→        .run(req.params.id, operator_id, admin.username);
   262→
   263→      return result;
   264→    });
   265→
   266→    const result = insertReportAndAttendance();
   267→
   268→    const report = db.prepare(`
   269→      SELECT sr.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, sr.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
   270→        r.short_name as repeater_name, r.bundesland_code as repeater_bundesland_code, r.is_linked as repeater_is_linked,
   271→        ep.abbreviation as einstiegspunkt_abbr, ep.site_name as einstiegspunkt_name
   272→      FROM signal_reports sr
   273→      JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   274→      JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
   275→      LEFT JOIN einstiegspunkte ep ON ep.id = sr.einstiegspunkt_id
   276→      WHERE sr.id = ?
   277→    `).get(result.lastInsertRowid);
   278→
   279→    res.status(201).json(report);
   280→  } catch (e: any) {
   281→    if (e.message?.includes('UNIQUE')) {
   282→      const existing = db.prepare(`
   283→        SELECT sr.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, sr.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
   284→          r.short_name as repeater_name, r.bundesland_code as repeater_bundesland_code, r.is_linked as repeater_is_linked,
   285→          ep.abbreviation as einstiegspunkt_abbr, ep.site_name as einstiegspunkt_name
   286→        FROM signal_reports sr
   287→        JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   288→        JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
   289→        LEFT JOIN einstiegspunkte ep ON ep.id = sr.einstiegspunkt_id
   290→        WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.operator_id = ? AND sr.repeater_id = ?
   291→      `).get(req.params.id, operator_id, repeater_id);
   292→      res.status(409).json({ error: 'Rapport für diesen Operator auf diesem Umsetzer existiert bereits', existing_report: existing });
   293→    } else {
   294→      throw e;
   295→    }
   296→  }
   297→});
   298→
   299→exercisesRouter.patch('/:id/reports/:rid', (req, res) => {
   300→  const db = getDb();

   302→  const updates: string[] = [];

     1→import { Router } from 'express';
     2→import { getDb } from '../db/database.js';
     3→
     4→export const reportsRouter = Router();
     5→
     6→// Aggregated stats across a date range
     7→reportsRouter.get('/stats', (req, res) => {
     8→  const { from, to, types } = req.query;
     9→  if (!from || !to) {
    10→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'from und to Parameter erforderlich (YYYY-MM-DD)' });
    11→    return;
    12→  }
    13→
    14→  const db = getDb();
    15→
    16→  // Fetch exercises in date range, optionally filtered by type
    17→  let typeFilter = '';
    18→  const queryParams: any[] = [from, to];
    19→  if (types && typeof types === 'string' && types.trim()) {
    20→    const typeList = types.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    21→    if (typeList.length > 0) {
    22→      typeFilter = ` AND e.exercise_type IN (${typeList.map(() => '?').join(',')})`;
    23→      queryParams.push(...typeList);
    24→    }
    25→  }
    26→
    27→  const exercises = db.prepare(
    28→    `SELECT e.id, e.date, e.name, e.oe_link_enabled, e.exercise_type,
    29→      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0) as participant_count,
    30→      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL) as report_count
    31→    FROM exercises e
    32→    WHERE e.date >= ? AND e.date <= ?${typeFilter}
    33→    ORDER BY e.date ASC`
    34→  ).all(...queryParams) as any[];
    35→
    36→  if (exercises.length === 0) {
    37→    res.json({
    38→      exercises: [],
    39→      stats: { totalParticipants: 0, totalReports: 0, perRepeater: [], bezirkStats: [], blStats: [], participants: [] },
    40→    });
    41→    return;
    42→  }
    43→
    44→  const ids = exercises.map((e: any) => e.id);
    45→  const placeholders = ids.map(() => '?').join(',');
    46→
    47→  const totalParticipants = (db.prepare(
    48→    `SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND is_op_marker = 0`
    49→  ).get(...ids) as any)?.c || 0;
    50→
    51→  const totalReports = (db.prepare(
    52→    `SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL`
    53→  ).get(...ids) as any)?.c || 0;
    54→
    55→  const perRepeater = db.prepare(
    56→    `SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count
    57→    FROM signal_reports sr
    58→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    59→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0 AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL
    60→    GROUP BY sr.repeater_id
    61→    ORDER BY count DESC`
    62→  ).all(...ids);
    63→
    64→  const bezirkStats = db.prepare(
    65→    `SELECT bl.name as bundesland, bl.code as bundesland_code, bl.sort_order as bl_sort,
    66→      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
    67→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
    68→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
    69→    FROM signal_reports sr
    70→    JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code
    71→    JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code
    72→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    73→    GROUP BY bz.code
    74→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code`
    75→  ).all(...ids);
    76→
    77→  const blStats = db.prepare(
    78→    `SELECT
    79→      COALESCE(bl.name, 'Sonstige') as bundesland,
    80→      o.bundesland_code,
    81→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
    82→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
    83→    FROM signal_reports sr
    84→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    85→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    86→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    87→    GROUP BY o.bundesland_code
    88→    ORDER BY o.bundesland_code`
    89→  ).all(...ids);
    90→
    91→  const participants = db.prepare(
    92→    `SELECT o.callsign, o.name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
    93→      bl.name as bundesland_name,
    94→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count
    95→    FROM signal_reports sr
    96→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    97→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    98→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    99→    GROUP BY sr.operator_id
   100→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign`
   101→  ).all(...ids);
   102→
   103→  res.json({
   104→    exercises,
   105→    stats: { totalParticipants, totalReports, perRepeater, bezirkStats, blStats, participants },
   106→  });
   107→});
   108→
   109→// ADIF export across a date range
   110→reportsRouter.get('/adif', (req, res) => {
   111→  const { from, to } = req.query;
   112→  if (!from || !to) {
   113→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'from und to Parameter erforderlich (YYYY-MM-DD)' });
   114→    return;
   115→  }
   116→
   117→  const db = getDb();
   118→
   119→  const exercises = db.prepare(
   120→    `SELECT id, date FROM exercises WHERE date >= ? AND date <= ? ORDER BY date ASC`
   121→  ).all(from, to) as { id: string; date: string }[];
   122→
   123→  if (exercises.length === 0) {
   124→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Keine Übungen im gewählten Zeitraum' });
   125→    return;
   126→  }
   127→
   128→  const ids = exercises.map(e => e.id);
   129→  const placeholders = ids.map(() => '?').join(',');
   130→
   131→  const reports = db.prepare(
   132→    `SELECT sr.readability, sr.strength, sr.db_over_s9, sr.notes, sr.created_at,
   133→      o.callsign, o.name as op_name, o.qth,
   134→      r.short_name, r.frequency_mhz, r.band, r.type,
   135→      e.date as exercise_date
   136→    FROM signal_reports sr
   137→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   138→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
   139→    JOIN exercises e ON e.id = sr.exercise_id
   140→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders})
   141→      AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   142→      AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL
   143→    ORDER BY e.date, sr.created_at`
   144→  ).all(...ids) as any[];
   145→
   146→  // Build ADIF
   147→  const adifField = (name: string, value: string) => {
   148→    if (!value) return '';
   149→    return `<${name}:${value.length}>${value} `;
   150→  };
   151→
   152→  let adif = '';
   153→
   154→  // Header
   155→  adif += 'ADIF Export from BOS-ARSA Log\n';
   156→  adif += `https://bosarsalog.oeradio.at/\n`;
   157→  adif += `Date range: ${from} to ${to}\n`;
   158→  adif += `QSO count: ${reports.length}\n`;
   159→  adif += '\n';
   160→  adif += adifField('ADIF_VER', '3.1.4');
   161→  adif += adifField('PROGRAMID', 'BOS-ARSA Log');
   162→  adif += adifField('PROGRAMVERSION', '1.0.5');
   163→  adif += '\n<EOH>\n\n';
   164→
   165→  // Records
   166→  for (const r of reports) {
   167→    const qsoDate = r.exercise_date.replace(/-/g, '');
   168→
   169→    // Extract time from created_at (format: "2026-02-16 14:30:00")
   170→    let timeOn = '0900'; // default
   171→    if (r.created_at) {
   172→      const timePart = r.created_at.split(' ')[1];
   173→      if (timePart) {
   174→        timeOn = timePart.replace(/:/g, '').slice(0, 4);
   175→      }
   176→    }
   177→
   178→    // RST: combine readability + strength + optional dB
   179→    const rst = `${r.readability}${r.strength}`;
   180→
   181→    // Frequency in MHz as string
   182→    const freq = r.frequency_mhz ? String(r.frequency_mhz) : '';
   183→
   184→    // Band mapping
   185→    let band = r.band || '';
   186→    if (!band && r.frequency_mhz) {
   187→      if (r.frequency_mhz >= 144 && r.frequency_mhz < 148) band = '2m';
   188→      else if (r.frequency_mhz >= 430 && r.frequency_mhz < 440) band = '70cm';
   189→      else if (r.frequency_mhz >= 1240 && r.frequency_mhz < 1300) band = '23cm';
   190→    }
   191→
   192→    adif += adifField('CALL', r.callsign);
   193→    adif += adifField('QSO_DATE', qsoDate);
   194→    adif += adifField('TIME_ON', timeOn);
   195→    adif += adifField('MODE', 'FM');
   196→    if (band) adif += adifField('BAND', band);
   197→    if (freq) adif += adifField('FREQ', freq);
   198→    adif += adifField('RST_SENT', rst);
   199→    adif += adifField('RST_RCVD', rst);
   200→    if (r.op_name) adif += adifField('NAME', r.op_name);
   201→    if (r.qth) adif += adifField('QTH', r.qth);
   202→    if (r.notes) adif += adifField('COMMENT', r.notes);
   203→    adif += '<EOR>\n';
   204→  }
   205→
   206→  const filename = `BOS-ARSA_QSOs_${from}_${to}.adi`;
   207→  res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/plain; charset=utf-8');
   208→  res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename="${filename}"`);
   209→  res.send(adif);
   210→});
   211→
   212→// Placeholder fallback
   213→reportsRouter.get('/', (_req, res) => {
   214→  res.json({ message: 'Use /api/v1/reports/stats?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD' });
   215→});
   216→

     1→import { useState, useEffect, useRef, type FormEvent } from 'react';

     6→const BUNDESLAND_NAMES: Record<string, string> = {
     7→  '01': 'OE1 Wien',
     8→  '02': 'OE2 Salzburg',
     9→  '03': 'OE3 Niederösterreich',
    10→  '04': 'OE4 Burgenland',
    11→  '05': 'OE5 Oberösterreich',
    12→  '06': 'OE6 Steiermark',

    19→interface Props {

    22→  reports: any[];
    23→  simplexBezirke?: boolean;
    24→  simplexBlCodes?: string;

    29→
    30→interface EntryForm {

    32→  operator: any;

    36→  opQth: string;
    37→  bezirkCode: string;
    38→}
    39→
    40→export default function LandMode({ exerciseId, repeaters, reports, simplexBezirke, simplexBlCodes, onReportCreated, onReportUpdated, onReportDeleted }: Props) {
    41→  const [collapsedReps, setCollapsedReps] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
    42→  const [opCallsigns, setOpCallsigns] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
    43→  const [bezirke, setBezirke] = useState<any[]>([]);
    44→  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    45→  const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '' });
    46→  const [dragReportId, setDragReportId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    47→
    48→  // Load bezirke + OP callsigns
    49→  useEffect(() => {
    50→    Promise.all([
    51→      apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bezirke'),
    52→      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters`),
    53→    ]).then(([bz, exReps]) => {
    54→      setBezirke(bz);
    55→      const ops: Record<number, string> = {};
    56→      for (const er of exReps) {
    57→        if (er.operator_callsign) {
    58→          ops[er.repeater_id] = er.operator_callsign;
    59→        }
    60→      }
    61→      setOpCallsigns(ops);
    62→    }).catch(() => {});
    63→
    64→    const socket = getSocket();
    65→    const handleOpCallsignUpdated = (data: { exercise_id: string; repeater_id: number; operator_callsign: string }) => {
    66→      if (data.exercise_id !== exerciseId) return;
    67→      setOpCallsigns(prev => ({ ...prev, [data.repeater_id]: data.operator_callsign || '' }));
    68→    };
    69→    socket.on('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated);
    70→    return () => { socket.off('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated); };
    71→  }, [exerciseId]);
    72→
    73→  // Group repeaters by Bundesland — exclude linked repeaters (those belong to BundMode)
    74→  const repeatersByBl: Record<string, any[]> = {};
    75→  const simplexRepeaters: any[] = [];
    76→  for (const r of repeaters) {
    77→    if (r.is_linked) continue;
    78→    if (r.type === 'simplex') {
    79→      simplexRepeaters.push(r);
    80→    } else {
    81→      const blCode = r.bundesland_code || '_other';
    82→      if (!repeatersByBl[blCode]) repeatersByBl[blCode] = [];
    83→      repeatersByBl[blCode].push(r);
    84→    }
    85→  }
    86→
    87→  const activeBlCodes = simplexBlCodes ? simplexBlCodes.split(',').filter(Boolean) : ['01', '02', '03', '04', '05', '06', '07', '08', '09'];
    88→
    89→  const sortedBlCodes = Object.keys(repeatersByBl).sort((a, b) => {
    90→    if (a === '_other') return 1;
    91→    if (b === '_other') return -1;
    92→    return a.localeCompare(b);
    93→  });
    94→
    95→  const toggleRepeater = (repId: number) => {
    96→    setCollapsedReps(prev => {
    97→      const next = new Set(prev);
    98→      if (next.has(repId)) next.delete(repId); else next.add(repId);
    99→      return next;
   100→    });
   101→  };
   102→
   103→  const saveOpCallsign = async (repeaterId: number, val: string) => {
   104→    try {
   105→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters`, {
   106→        method: 'POST',
   107→        body: JSON.stringify({ repeater_id: repeaterId, operator_callsign: val || null }),
   108→      });
   109→    } catch {
   110→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters/${repeaterId}`, {
   111→        method: 'PATCH',
   112→        body: JSON.stringify({ operator_callsign: val || null }),
   113→      });
   114→    }
   115→    getSocket().emit('op_callsign_updated', { exercise_id: exerciseId, repeater_id: repeaterId, operator_callsign: val });
   116→  };
   117→
   118→  const parseRapport = (raw: string) => {
   119→    const match = raw.match(/^(\d)\/(\d)(.*)$/);
   120→    if (!match) return { readability: null, strength: null, db_over_s9: null };
   121→    return {
   122→      readability: parseInt(match[1]),
   123→      strength: parseInt(match[2]),
   124→      db_over_s9: match[3]?.trim() || null,
   125→    };
   126→  };
   127→
   128→  const handleBezirkSubmit = async (bezirkCode: string, repeaterId: number, form: EntryForm) => {
   129→    try {
   130→      const operator = await getOrCreateOperator(form.callsign, form.operator);
   131→      if (!operator) return;
   132→
   133→      const parsed = parseRapport(form.rapport);
   134→
   135→      try {
   136→        const report = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {
   137→          method: 'POST',
   138→          body: JSON.stringify({
   139→            operator_id: operator.id,
   140→            repeater_id: repeaterId,
   141→            ...parsed,
   142→            notes: form.notes || null,
   143→            bezirk_code: (bezirkCode && bezirkCode !== '??') ? bezirkCode : null,
   144→          }),

   146→        onReportCreated(report);
   147→      } catch (err: any) {
   148→        if (err.message?.includes('existiert') && err.data?.existing_report) {
   149→          const existing = err.data.existing_report;
   150→          const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${existing.id}`, {
   151→            method: 'PATCH',
   152→            body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: form.notes || null }),
   153→          });
   154→          onReportUpdated(updated);
   155→        }
   156→      }
   157→    } catch {}
   158→  };
   159→
   160→  const handleEdit = (report: any) => {

   162→    setEditForm({
   163→      callsign: report.callsign,
   164→      operator: { id: report.operator_id, callsign: report.callsign, name: report.operator_name || null, qth: report.operator_qth || null },
   165→      rapport: report.readability && report.strength ? `${report.readability}/${report.strength}${report.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '',
   166→      notes: report.notes || '',
   167→      opName: report.operator_name || '',
   168→      opQth: report.operator_qth || '',
   169→      bezirkCode: report.bezirk_code || '',
   170→    });
   171→  };
   172→
   173→  const handleUpdate = async () => {
   174→    if (!editingId) return;
   175→    const parsed = parseRapport(editForm.rapport);
   176→    try {
   177→      // Save operator name/qth if changed
   178→      if (editForm.operator?.id) {
   179→        const nameChanged = (editForm.opName || '') !== (editForm.operator.name || '');
   180→        const qthChanged = (editForm.opQth || '') !== (editForm.operator.qth || '');
   181→        if (nameChanged || qthChanged) {
   182→          const patch: any = {};
   183→          if (nameChanged) patch.name = editForm.opName || null;
   184→          if (qthChanged) patch.qth = editForm.opQth || null;
   185→          await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${editForm.operator.id}`, {
   186→            method: 'PATCH',
   187→            body: JSON.stringify(patch),
   188→          });
   189→        }
   190→      }
   191→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${editingId}`, {
   192→        method: 'PATCH',
   193→        body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: editForm.notes || null, bezirk_code: editForm.bezirkCode || null }),
   194→      });
   195→      // Update the returned report with new name/qth so display refreshes
   196→      if (editForm.opName !== undefined) updated.operator_name = editForm.opName || null;
   197→      if (editForm.opQth !== undefined) updated.operator_qth = editForm.opQth || null;
   198→      onReportUpdated(updated);
   199→      setEditingId(null);
   200→    } catch {}
   201→  };
   202→
   203→  const handleMoveToBezirk = async (reportId: number, newBezirkCode: string | null) => {
   204→    try {
   205→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, {
   206→        method: 'PATCH',
   207→        body: JSON.stringify({ bezirk_code: newBezirkCode }),
   208→      });
   209→      onReportUpdated(updated);
   210→    } catch {}
   211→  };
   212→
   213→  const handleDelete = async (reportId: number) => {
   214→    if (!confirm('Rapport löschen?')) return;
   215→    try {
   216→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
   217→      onReportDeleted(reportId);
   218→    } catch {}
   219→  };
   220→
   221→  const renderRepeaterCard = (rep: any) => {
   222→    const repReports = reports.filter(r => r.repeater_id === rep.repeater_id && !r.is_op_marker);
   223→    const reportCount = repReports.filter(r => r.readability).length;
   224→    const isCollapsed = collapsedReps.has(rep.repeater_id);
   225→    const isSimplexGrouped = simplexBezirke && rep.type === 'simplex';
   226→    const repBezirke = isSimplexGrouped
   227→      ? bezirke.filter(b => activeBlCodes.includes(b.bundesland_code))
   228→      : bezirke.filter(b => b.bundesland_code === rep.bundesland_code);
   229→
   230→    // Group bezirke by BL for simplex grouped rendering
   231→    const bezirkeByBl: Record<string, any[]> = {};
   232→    if (isSimplexGrouped) {
   233→      for (const bz of repBezirke) {
   234→        if (!bezirkeByBl[bz.bundesland_code]) bezirkeByBl[bz.bundesland_code] = [];
   235→        bezirkeByBl[bz.bundesland_code].push(bz);
   236→      }
   237→    }
   238→
   239→    return (
   240→      <div key={rep.repeater_id} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm">
   241→        <div className="bg-gray-50 px-2 sm:px-3 py-2 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between border-b gap-1">
   242→          <div
   243→            onClick={() => toggleRepeater(rep.repeater_id)}
   244→            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100 rounded px-1 -mx-1 min-w-0"
   245→          >
   246→            <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">{isCollapsed ? '▶' : '▼'}</span>
   247→            <span className="font-medium text-xs sm:text-sm truncate">{rep.short_name}{rep.repeater_callsign ? ` (${rep.repeater_callsign})` : ''}</span>
   248→            <span className="bg-[#0d6efd] text-white text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full flex-shrink-0">{reportCount}</span>
   249→            {rep.frequency_mhz && (
   250→              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 hidden sm:inline">{rep.frequency_mhz} MHz</span>
   251→            )}
   252→            {rep.offset_mhz && (
   253→              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 hidden md:inline">({rep.offset_mhz > 0 ? '+' : ''}{rep.offset_mhz})</span>
   254→            )}
   255→            {rep.ctcss_hz && (
   256→              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 hidden md:inline">CTCSS {rep.ctcss_hz}</span>
   257→            )}
   258→          </div>
   259→          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
   260→            <label className={`text-xs ${opCallsigns[rep.repeater_id] ? 'text-gray-500' : 'text-[#c8102e] font-semibold'}`}>OP:</label>
   261→            <input
   262→              type="text"
   263→              value={opCallsigns[rep.repeater_id] || ''}
   264→              onChange={e => setOpCallsigns(prev => ({ ...prev, [rep.repeater_id]: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   265→              onBlur={e => saveOpCallsign(rep.repeater_id, e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
   266→              placeholder="Rufz."
   267→              className={`border rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono uppercase w-20 sm:w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 ${opCallsigns[rep.repeater_id] ? 'border-gray-300' : 'border-[#c8102e] placeholder-[#c8102e]/60'}`}
   268→            />
   269→          </div>
   270→        </div>
   271→
   272→        {!isCollapsed && (
   273→          <div className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   274→            {repBezirke.length > 0 ? (
   275→              <>
   276→                {isSimplexGrouped ? (
   277→                  /* Simplex grouped by BL → Bezirk */
   278→                  <>
   279→                    {activeBlCodes.sort().map(blCode => {
   280→                      const blBezirke = bezirkeByBl[blCode] || [];
   281→                      if (blBezirke.length === 0) return null;
   282→                      const blLabel = BUNDESLAND_NAMES[blCode] || blCode;
   283→                      return (
   284→                        <div key={blCode}>
   285→                          <div className="bg-gray-100 px-2 sm:px-3 py-1">
   286→                            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">{blLabel}</span>
   287→                          </div>
   288→                          {blBezirke.map(bz => {
   289→                            const bzReports = repReports.filter(r => r.bezirk_code === bz.code);
   290→                            return (
   291→                              <BezirkRow
   292→                                key={bz.code}
   293→                                bezirk={bz}
   294→                                reports={bzReports}
   295→                                repeaterId={rep.repeater_id}
   296→                                availableBezirke={repBezirke}
   297→                                onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit(bz.code, rep.repeater_id, form)}
   298→                                onEdit={handleEdit}
   299→                                onDelete={handleDelete}
   300→                                onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   301→                                editingId={editingId}
   302→                                editForm={editForm}
   303→                                onEditChange={setEditForm}
   304→                                onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   305→                                onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   306→                                dragReportId={dragReportId}
   307→                                onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   308→                                onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   309→                              />
   310→                            );
   311→                          })}
   312→                        </div>
   313→                      );
   314→                    })}
   315→                    {/* Sonstige — reports without matching bezirk */}
   316→                    {(() => {
   317→                      const bzCodes = new Set(repBezirke.map(b => b.code));
   318→                      const unassigned = repReports.filter(r => !r.bezirk_code || !bzCodes.has(r.bezirk_code));
   319→                      return (
   320→                        <BezirkRow
   321→                          bezirk={{ code: '??', name: 'Sonstige', is_capital: false }}
   322→                          reports={unassigned}
   323→                          repeaterId={rep.repeater_id}
   324→                          availableBezirke={repBezirke}
   325→                          onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit('??', rep.repeater_id, form)}
   326→                          onEdit={handleEdit}
   327→                          onDelete={handleDelete}
   328→                          onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   329→                          editingId={editingId}
   330→                          editForm={editForm}
   331→                          onEditChange={setEditForm}
   332→                          onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   333→                          onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   334→                          dragReportId={dragReportId}
   335→                          onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   336→                          onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   337→                        />
   338→                      );
   339→                    })()}
   340→                  </>
   341→                ) : (
   342→                  /* Normal repeater with bezirke */
   343→                  <>
   344→                    {repBezirke.map(bz => {
   345→                      const bzReports = repReports.filter(r => r.bezirk_code === bz.code);
   346→                      return (
   347→                        <BezirkRow
   348→                          key={bz.code}
   349→                          bezirk={bz}
   350→                          reports={bzReports}
   351→                          repeaterId={rep.repeater_id}
   352→                          availableBezirke={repBezirke}
   353→                          onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit(bz.code, rep.repeater_id, form)}
   354→                          onEdit={handleEdit}
   355→                          onDelete={handleDelete}
   356→                          onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   357→                          editingId={editingId}
   358→                          editForm={editForm}
   359→                          onEditChange={setEditForm}
   360→                          onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   361→                          onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   362→                          dragReportId={dragReportId}
   363→                          onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   364→                          onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   365→                        />
   366→                      );
   367→                    })}
   368→                    {/* Sonstige — reports without matching bezirk + entry form */}
   369→                    {(() => {
   370→                      const bzCodes = new Set(repBezirke.map(b => b.code));
   371→                      const unassigned = repReports.filter(r => !r.bezirk_code || !bzCodes.has(r.bezirk_code));
   372→                      return (
   373→                        <BezirkRow
   374→                          bezirk={{ code: '??', name: 'Sonstige', is_capital: false }}
   375→                          reports={unassigned}
   376→                          repeaterId={rep.repeater_id}
   377→                          availableBezirke={repBezirke}
   378→                          onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit('??', rep.repeater_id, form)}
   379→                          onEdit={handleEdit}
   380→                          onDelete={handleDelete}
   381→                          onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   382→                          editingId={editingId}
   383→                          editForm={editForm}
   384→                          onEditChange={setEditForm}
   385→                          onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   386→                          onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   387→                          dragReportId={dragReportId}
   388→                          onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   389→                          onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   390→                        />
   391→                      );
   392→                    })()}
   393→                  </>
   394→                )}
   395→              </>
   396→            ) : (
   397→              /* No Bezirke — flat list with input form */
   398→              <FlatReportRow
   399→                reports={repReports}
   400→                repeaterId={rep.repeater_id}
   401→                onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit('', rep.repeater_id, form)}
   402→                onEdit={handleEdit}
   403→                onDelete={handleDelete}
   404→                editingId={editingId}
   405→                editForm={editForm}
   406→                onEditChange={setEditForm}
   407→                onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   408→                onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   409→              />
   410→            )}
   411→          </div>
   412→        )}
   413→      </div>
   414→    );
   415→  };
   416→
   417→  return (
   418→    <div className="space-y-2">
   419→      {sortedBlCodes.map(blCode => {
   420→        const blReps = repeatersByBl[blCode];
   421→        const label = BUNDESLAND_NAMES[blCode] || 'Sonstige';
   422→        return (
   423→          <div key={blCode}>
   424→            <div className="px-1 mb-1 mt-2">
   425→              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">{label}</span>
   426→            </div>
   427→            <div className="space-y-2">
   428→              {blReps.map(rep => renderRepeaterCard(rep))}
   429→            </div>
   430→          </div>
   431→        );
   432→      })}
   433→
   434→      {simplexRepeaters.length > 0 && (
   435→        <div>
   436→          <div className="px-1 mb-1 mt-2">
   437→            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">Simplex</span>
   438→          </div>
   439→          <div className="space-y-2">
   440→            {simplexRepeaters.map(rep => renderRepeaterCard(rep))}
   441→          </div>
   442→        </div>
   443→      )}
   444→    </div>
   445→  );
   446→}
   447→
   448→interface BezirkRowProps {
   449→  bezirk: any;
   450→  reports: any[];
   451→  repeaterId: number;
   452→  availableBezirke: any[];
   453→  onSubmit: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   454→  onEdit: (report: any) => void;
   455→  onDelete: (id: number) => void;
   456→  onMoveToBezirk: (reportId: number, bezirkCode: string | null) => void;
   457→  editingId: number | null;
   458→  editForm: EntryForm;
   459→  onEditChange: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   460→  onEditSave: () => void;
   461→  onEditCancel: () => void;
   462→  dragReportId: number | null;
   463→  onDragStart: (reportId: number) => void;
   464→  onDragEnd: () => void;
   465→}
   466→
   467→function BezirkRow({ bezirk, reports, repeaterId, availableBezirke, onSubmit, onEdit, onDelete, onMoveToBezirk, editingId, editForm, onEditChange, onEditSave, onEditCancel, dragReportId, onDragStart, onDragEnd }: BezirkRowProps) {
   468→  const [form, setForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '' });
   469→  const [dragOver, setDragOver] = useState(false);
   470→  const callsignRef = useRef<CallsignInputRef>(null);
   471→  const rapportRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   472→
   473→  const handleSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
   474→    e.preventDefault();
   475→    if (!form.callsign && !form.operator) return;
   476→    onSubmit(form);
   477→    setForm({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '' });
   478→    setTimeout(() => callsignRef.current?.focus(), 50);
   479→  };
   480→
   481→  const handleDrop = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
   482→    e.preventDefault();
   483→    setDragOver(false);
   484→    if (dragReportId) {
   485→      const targetCode = bezirk.code === '??' ? null : bezirk.code;
   486→      onMoveToBezirk(dragReportId, targetCode);
   487→    }
   488→  };
   489→
   490→  const saveOperatorField = (field: 'name' | 'qth', value: string) => {
   491→    if (!form.operator?.id) return;
   492→    const original = field === 'name' ? (form.operator.name || '') : (form.operator.qth || '');
   493→    if (value === original) return;
   494→    apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${form.operator.id}`, {
   495→      method: 'PATCH',
   496→      body: JSON.stringify({ [field]: value || null }),
   497→    }).then(() => {
   498→      setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: { ...f.operator, [field]: value || null } }));
   499→    }).catch(() => {});
   500→  };
   501→
   502→  return (
   503→    <div
   504→      className={`px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors ${dragOver && dragReportId ? 'bg-blue-100 ring-2 ring-blue-400 ring-inset' : ''}`}
   505→      onDragOver={e => { if (dragReportId) { e.preventDefault(); setDragOver(true); } }}
   506→      onDragLeave={() => setDragOver(false)}
   507→      onDrop={handleDrop}
   508→    >
   509→      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 mb-1">
   510→        <span className={`text-xs font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-white ${bezirk.code === '??' ? 'bg-[#ffc107] text-gray-800' : bezirk.is_capital ? 'bg-[#dc3545]' : 'bg-[#6c757d]'}`}>
   511→          {bezirk.code === '??' ? '??' : bezirk.code}
   512→        </span>
   513→        <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 truncate">{bezirk.name}</span>
   514→        {reports.length > 0 && <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0">({reports.length})</span>}
   515→      </div>
   516→
   517→      {reports.length > 0 && (
   518→        <table className="w-full text-xs mb-1">
   519→          <tbody>
   520→            {reports.map((r, idx) => (
   521→              <tr
   522→                key={r.id}
   523→                className={`hover:bg-blue-50 group ${dragReportId === r.id ? 'opacity-40' : ''}`}
   524→                draggable={editingId !== r.id}
   525→                onDragStart={e => { e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move'; onDragStart(r.id); }}
   526→                onDragEnd={onDragEnd}
   527→              >
   528→                {editingId === r.id ? (
   529→                  <td colSpan={5} className="py-0.5">
   530→                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-blue-50 rounded p-1 flex-wrap" onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); onEditSave(); } }}>
   531→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}</span>
   532→                      <input value={editForm.opName} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opName: e.target.value })} placeholder="Name" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 hidden sm:block" />
   533→                      <input value={editForm.opQth} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opQth: e.target.value })} placeholder="QTH" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-14 sm:w-16 hidden sm:block" />
   534→                      <input value={editForm.rapport} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" autoFocus />
   535→                      <input value={editForm.notes} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, notes: e.target.value })} placeholder="Sonst." className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" />
   536→                      <select
   537→                        value={editForm.bezirkCode}
   538→                        onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, bezirkCode: e.target.value })}
   539→                        className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-14 sm:w-16"
   540→                        title="Bezirk"
   541→                      >
   542→                        <option value="">??</option>
   543→                        {availableBezirke.map(bz => <option key={bz.code} value={bz.code}>{bz.code}</option>)}
   544→                      </select>
   545→                      <button onClick={onEditSave} className="text-green-600 text-xs font-bold">OK</button>
   546→                      <button onClick={onEditCancel} className="text-gray-400 text-xs">X</button>
   547→                    </div>
   548→                  </td>
   549→                ) : (
   550→                  <>
   551→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-400 w-4 cursor-grab">{idx + 1}</td>
   552→                    <td className="py-0.5 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   553→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}</span>
   554→                      {r.operator_name && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-500 text-xs">{r.operator_name}</span>}
   555→                      {r.operator_qth && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-400 text-xs hidden sm:inline">{r.operator_qth}</span>}
   556→                    </td>
   557→                    <td className="py-0.5 font-mono text-gray-500 w-16 sm:w-24 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   558→                      {r.readability && r.strength ? `${r.readability}/${r.strength}${r.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '—'}
   559→                    </td>
   560→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-500 cursor-pointer hidden sm:table-cell" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>{r.notes || ''}</td>
   561→                    <td className="py-0.5 w-4">
   562→                      <button onClick={() => onDelete(r.id)} className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100">x</button>
   563→                    </td>
   564→                  </>
   565→                )}
   566→              </tr>
   567→            ))}
   568→          </tbody>
   569→        </table>
   570→      )}
   571→
   572→      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex items-center gap-1">
   573→        <CallsignInput
   574→          ref={callsignRef}
   575→          value={form.callsign}
   576→          onChange={v => setForm(f => ({ ...f, callsign: v, operator: null, opName: '', opQth: '' }))}
   577→          onSelect={op => {
   578→            setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: op, callsign: op.callsign, opName: op.name || '', opQth: op.qth || '' }));
   579→            setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50);
   580→          }}
   581→          onEnter={() => setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50)}
   582→          className="w-24 sm:w-28"
   583→        />
   584→        <input
   585→          type="text"
   586→          value={form.opName}
   587→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opName: e.target.value }))}
   588→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('name', e.target.value)}
   589→          placeholder="Name"
   590→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-20 sm:w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   591→        />
   592→        <input
   593→          type="text"
   594→          value={form.opQth}
   595→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opQth: e.target.value }))}
   596→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('qth', e.target.value)}
   597→          placeholder="QTH"
   598→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   599→        />
   600→        <input
   601→          ref={rapportRef}
   602→          type="text"
   603→          value={form.rapport}
   604→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   605→          placeholder="5/9"
   606→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   607→          onKeyDown={e => {
   608→            if (e.altKey && e.key >= '1' && e.key <= '9') {
   609→              e.preventDefault();
   610→              setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: `5/${e.key}` }));
   611→            }
   612→          }}
   613→        />
   614→        <input
   615→          type="text"
   616→          value={form.notes}
   617→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, notes: e.target.value }))}
   618→          placeholder="Sonst."
   619→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs flex-1 min-w-[40px] sm:min-w-[60px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   620→        />
   621→        <button type="submit" title="Rapport speichern (Enter)" className="bg-[#c8102e] hover:bg-[#a00d24] text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs flex-shrink-0">
   622→          + ↵
   623→        </button>
   624→      </form>
   625→    </div>
   626→  );
   627→}
   628→
   629→interface FlatReportRowProps {
   630→  reports: any[];
   631→  repeaterId: number;
   632→  onSubmit: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   633→  onEdit: (report: any) => void;
   634→  onDelete: (id: number) => void;
   635→  editingId: number | null;
   636→  editForm: EntryForm;
   637→  onEditChange: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   638→  onEditSave: () => void;
   639→  onEditCancel: () => void;
   640→}
   641→
   642→function FlatReportRow({ reports, repeaterId, onSubmit, onEdit, onDelete, editingId, editForm, onEditChange, onEditSave, onEditCancel }: FlatReportRowProps) {
   643→  const [form, setForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '' });
   644→  const callsignRef = useRef<CallsignInputRef>(null);
   645→  const rapportRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   646→
   647→  const handleSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
   648→    e.preventDefault();
   649→    if (!form.callsign && !form.operator) return;
   650→    onSubmit(form);
   651→    setForm({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '' });
   652→    setTimeout(() => callsignRef.current?.focus(), 50);
   653→  };
   654→
   655→  const saveOperatorField = (field: 'name' | 'qth', value: string) => {
   656→    if (!form.operator?.id) return;
   657→    const original = field === 'name' ? (form.operator.name || '') : (form.operator.qth || '');
   658→    if (value === original) return;
   659→    apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${form.operator.id}`, {
   660→      method: 'PATCH',
   661→      body: JSON.stringify({ [field]: value || null }),
   662→    }).then(() => {
   663→      setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: { ...f.operator, [field]: value || null } }));
   664→    }).catch(() => {});
   665→  };
   666→
   667→  return (
   668→    <div className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5">
   669→      {reports.length > 0 && (
   670→        <table className="w-full text-xs mb-1">
   671→          <tbody>
   672→            {reports.map((r, idx) => (
   673→              <tr key={r.id} className="hover:bg-blue-50 group">
   674→                {editingId === r.id ? (
   675→                  <td colSpan={5} className="py-0.5">
   676→                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-blue-50 rounded p-1 flex-wrap" onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); onEditSave(); } }}>
   677→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}</span>
   678→                      <input value={editForm.opName} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opName: e.target.value })} placeholder="Name" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 hidden sm:block" />
   679→                      <input value={editForm.opQth} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opQth: e.target.value })} placeholder="QTH" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-14 sm:w-16 hidden sm:block" />
   680→                      <input value={editForm.rapport} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" autoFocus />
   681→                      <input value={editForm.notes} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, notes: e.target.value })} placeholder="Sonst." className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" />
   682→                      <button onClick={onEditSave} className="text-green-600 text-xs font-bold">OK</button>
   683→                      <button onClick={onEditCancel} className="text-gray-400 text-xs">X</button>
   684→                    </div>
   685→                  </td>
   686→                ) : (
   687→                  <>
   688→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-400 w-4">{idx + 1}</td>
   689→                    <td className="py-0.5 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   690→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}</span>
   691→                      {r.operator_name && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-500 text-xs">{r.operator_name}</span>}
   692→                      {r.operator_qth && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-400 text-xs hidden sm:inline">{r.operator_qth}</span>}
   693→                    </td>
   694→                    <td className="py-0.5 font-mono text-gray-500 w-16 sm:w-24 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   695→                      {r.readability && r.strength ? `${r.readability}/${r.strength}${r.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '—'}
   696→                    </td>
   697→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-500 cursor-pointer hidden sm:table-cell" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>{r.notes || ''}</td>
   698→                    <td className="py-0.5 w-4">
   699→                      <button onClick={() => onDelete(r.id)} className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100">x</button>
   700→                    </td>
   701→                  </>
   702→                )}
   703→              </tr>
   704→            ))}
   705→          </tbody>
   706→        </table>
   707→      )}
   708→
   709→      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex items-center gap-1">
   710→        <CallsignInput
   711→          ref={callsignRef}
   712→          value={form.callsign}
   713→          onChange={v => setForm(f => ({ ...f, callsign: v, operator: null, opName: '', opQth: '' }))}
   714→          onSelect={op => {
   715→            setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: op, callsign: op.callsign, opName: op.name || '', opQth: op.qth || '' }));
   716→            setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50);
   717→          }}
   718→          onEnter={() => setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50)}
   719→          className="w-24 sm:w-28"
   720→        />
   721→        <input
   722→          type="text"
   723→          value={form.opName}
   724→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opName: e.target.value }))}
   725→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('name', e.target.value)}
   726→          placeholder="Name"
   727→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-20 sm:w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   728→        />
   729→        <input
   730→          type="text"
   731→          value={form.opQth}
   732→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opQth: e.target.value }))}
   733→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('qth', e.target.value)}
   734→          placeholder="QTH"
   735→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   736→        />
   737→        <input
   738→          ref={rapportRef}
   739→          type="text"
   740→          value={form.rapport}
   741→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   742→          placeholder="5/9"
   743→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   744→          onKeyDown={e => {
   745→            if (e.altKey && e.key >= '1' && e.key <= '9') {
   746→              e.preventDefault();
   747→              setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: `5/${e.key}` }));
   748→            }
   749→          }}
   750→        />
   751→        <input
   752→          type="text"
   753→          value={form.notes}
   754→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, notes: e.target.value }))}
   755→          placeholder="Sonst."
   756→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs flex-1 min-w-[40px] sm:min-w-[60px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   757→        />
   758→        <button type="submit" title="Rapport speichern (Enter)" className="bg-[#c8102e] hover:bg-[#a00d24] text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs flex-shrink-0">
   759→          + ↵
   760→        </button>
   761→      </form>
   762→    </div>
   763→  );
   764→}

    31→  const createExportHeader = () => {

    34→    header.style.cssText = 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:16px 24px;background:#1e3a5f;border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:16px;';

    50→  };
    51→
    52→  const createExportFooter = () => {

    57→    return footer;

    59→
    60→  const downloadChart = async (cardRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>, filename: string) => {
    61→    if (!cardRef.current || !exercise) return;
    62→    setExporting(true);
    63→    const header = createExportHeader();
    64→    const footer = createExportFooter();
    65→    // Wrap the card temporarily
    66→    const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
    67→    wrapper.style.cssText = 'padding:24px;background:#f3f4f6;';
    68→    const parent = cardRef.current.parentNode!;
    69→    const next = cardRef.current.nextSibling;
    70→    parent.insertBefore(wrapper, cardRef.current);

    72→    wrapper.appendChild(cardRef.current);

    74→    try {

    78→      link.href = url;
    79→      link.click();
    80→    } catch {} finally {
    81→      // Move card back to original position
    82→      if (next) parent.insertBefore(cardRef.current, next); else parent.appendChild(cardRef.current);
    83→      wrapper.remove();

    85→    }
    86→  };

   383→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalReports}</td>
   384→                  </tr>
   385→                </tbody>
   386→              </table>
   387→              </div>
   388→            </div>
   389→          )}
   390→
   391→          {/* Participants list */}
   392→          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden">
   393→            <div
   394→              onClick={() => setShowParticipants(v => !v)}
   395→              className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold cursor-pointer hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] flex items-center justify-between"
   396→            >
   397→              <span>Teilnehmer-Liste ({stats.participants.length})</span>
   398→              <span className="text-xs">{showParticipants ? '▼' : '▶'}</span>
   399→            </div>
   400→            {showParticipants && (
   401→              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
   402→              <table className="w-full text-xs sm:text-sm min-w-[360px]">
   403→                <thead>
   404→                  <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b text-left text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">
   405→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 w-8">#</th>
   406→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Rufzeichen</th>
   407→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 hidden sm:table-cell">Name</th>
   408→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Bezirk</th>
   409→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 hidden md:table-cell">Bundesland / Land</th>
   410→                    <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-16 sm:w-24">Rapp.</th>
   411→                  </tr>
   412→                </thead>
   413→                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   414→                  {stats.participants.map((p, idx) => (
   415→                    <tr key={p.callsign} className="hover:bg-blue-50">
   416→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-xs text-gray-400">{idx + 1}</td>
   417→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 font-mono font-medium">{p.callsign}</td>
   418→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-gray-600 hidden sm:table-cell">{p.name || ''}</td>
   419→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1">
   420→                        {p.bezirk_code && (
   421→                          <span className="text-xs bg-gray-200 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono">{p.bezirk_code}</span>
   422→                        )}
   423→                      </td>
   424→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-gray-600 text-xs hidden md:table-cell">{p.bundesland_name || ''}</td>
   425→                      <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-right font-mono">{p.report_count}</td>
   426→                    </tr>
   427→                  ))}
   428→                </tbody>
   429→              </table>
   430→              </div>
   431→            )}
   432→          </div>
   433→
   434→          {/* Export buttons */}
   435→          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4" data-no-export="true">
   436→            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f] mb-3">Export</h2>
   437→            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
   438→              <button
   439→                onClick={downloadFullPage}
   440→                disabled={exporting}
   441→                className="bg-[#1e3a5f] hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium disabled:opacity-50"
   442→              >
   443→                {exporting ? 'Exportieren...' : 'PDF — Download Auswertung'}
   444→              </button>
   445→              {exercise.oe_link_enabled !== 0 && (
   446→                <a
   447→                  href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/bund`}
   448→                  className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   449→                  target="_blank"
   450→                >
   451→                  TXT — OE-Link
   452→                </a>
   453→              )}
   454→              <a
   455→                href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/land`}
   456→                className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   457→                target="_blank"
   458→              >
   459→                TXT — Frequenzen
   460→              </a>
   461→              <a
   462→                href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/combined`}
   463→                className="bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 text-red-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   464→                target="_blank"
   465→              >
   466→                TXT — Kombiniert
   467→              </a>
   468→            </div>
   469→          </div>
   470→        </>
   471→      )}
   472→    </div>
   473→  );
   474→}
   475→
