     1→import { useState, useEffect, useRef, type FormEvent } from 'react';
     2→import { apiFetch, getOrCreateOperator } from '../services/api';
     3→import { getSocket } from '../services/socket';
     4→import CallsignInput, { type CallsignInputRef } from './CallsignInput';
     5→
     6→interface Props {
     7→  exerciseId: string;
     8→  reports: any[];
     9→  onReportCreated: (report: any) => void;
    10→  onReportUpdated: (report: any) => void;
    11→  onReportDeleted: (reportId: number) => void;
    12→}
    13→
    14→interface EntryForm {
    15→  callsign: string;
    16→  operator: any;
    17→  rapport: string;
    18→  notes: string;
    19→  opName: string;
    20→  opQth: string;
    21→  bezirkCode: string;
    22→}
    23→
    24→export default function BundMode({ exerciseId, reports, onReportCreated, onReportUpdated, onReportDeleted }: Props) {
    25→  const [bundeslaender, setBundeslaender] = useState<any[]>([]);
    26→  const [bezirke, setBezirke] = useState<any[]>([]);
    27→  const [linkedRepeaters, setLinkedRepeaters] = useState<any[]>([]);
    28→  const [blRepSelection, setBlRepSelection] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
    29→  const [blOpCallsigns, setBlOpCallsigns] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
    30→  const [collapsedBl, setCollapsedBl] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
    31→  const [addRepBl, setAddRepBl] = useState<string | null>(null);
    32→  const [newRepName, setNewRepName] = useState('');
    33→  const [newRepFreq, setNewRepFreq] = useState('');
    34→  const [newRepCallsign, setNewRepCallsign] = useState('');
    35→  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    36→  const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '' });
    37→  const [dragReportId, setDragReportId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    38→
    39→  useEffect(() => {
    40→    Promise.all([
    41→      apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bundeslaender'),
    42→      apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bezirke'),
    43→      apiFetch('/api/v1/repeaters'),
    44→      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters`),
    45→    ]).then(([bl, bz, allReps, exReps]) => {
    46→      setBundeslaender(bl);
    47→      setBezirke(bz);
    48→      const linked = allReps.filter((r: any) => r.is_linked);
    49→      setLinkedRepeaters(linked);
    50→      // Initialize per-BL defaults: first linked in that BL, else first overall
    51→      const defaults: Record<string, number> = {};
    52→      const ops: Record<string, string> = {};
    53→      for (const b of bl) {
    54→        const inBl = linked.find((r: any) => r.bundesland_code === b.code);
    55→        const repId = inBl ? inBl.id : (linked[0]?.id ?? 0);
    56→        defaults[b.code] = repId;
    57→        // Load existing OP callsign from exercise_repeaters
    58→        const exRep = exReps.find((er: any) => er.repeater_id === repId);
    59→        ops[b.code] = exRep?.operator_callsign || '';
    60→      }
    61→      setBlRepSelection(defaults);
    62→      setBlOpCallsigns(ops);
    63→    }).catch(() => {});
    64→
    65→    const socket = getSocket();
    66→    const handleOpCallsignUpdated = (data: { exercise_id: string; repeater_id: number; operator_callsign: string }) => {
    67→      if (data.exercise_id !== exerciseId) return;
    68→      // Map repeater_id back to blCode via current blRepSelection
    69→      setBlRepSelection(sel => {
    70→        for (const [blCode, repId] of Object.entries(sel)) {
    71→          if (repId === data.repeater_id) {
    72→            setBlOpCallsigns(prev => ({ ...prev, [blCode]: data.operator_callsign || '' }));
    73→          }
    74→        }
    75→        return sel;
    76→      });
    77→    };
    78→    socket.on('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated);
    79→    return () => { socket.off('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated); };
    80→  }, [exerciseId]);
    81→
    82→  const saveOpCallsign = async (blCode: string, val: string) => {
    83→    const repId = blRepSelection[blCode];
    84→    if (!repId) return;
    85→    try {
    86→      // Ensure exercise_repeater exists, then update
    87→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters`, {
    88→        method: 'POST',
    89→        body: JSON.stringify({ repeater_id: repId, operator_callsign: val || null }),
    90→      });
    91→    } catch {
    92→      // Already exists, just patch
    93→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters/${repId}`, {
    94→        method: 'PATCH',
    95→        body: JSON.stringify({ operator_callsign: val || null }),
    96→      });
    97→    }
    98→    getSocket().emit('op_callsign_updated', { exercise_id: exerciseId, repeater_id: repId, operator_callsign: val });
    99→  };
   100→
   101→  const addRepeater = async (blCode: string) => {
   102→    if (!newRepName && !newRepFreq) return;
   103→    const name = newRepName || `Direkte ${newRepFreq}`;
   104→    const freq = newRepFreq ? parseFloat(newRepFreq) : null;
   105→    try {
   106→      const rep = await apiFetch('/api/v1/repeaters', {
   107→        method: 'POST',
   108→        body: JSON.stringify({
   109→          short_name: name,
   110→          site_name: name,
   111→          frequency_mhz: freq,
   112→          callsign: newRepCallsign || null,
   113→          band: freq ? (freq > 400 ? '70cm' : freq > 200 ? '23cm' : '2m') : null,
   114→          type: 'repeater',
   115→          bundesland_code: blCode,
   116→        }),
   117→      });
   118→      setLinkedRepeaters(prev => [...prev, rep]);
   119→      setNewRepName('');
   120→      setNewRepFreq('');
   121→      setNewRepCallsign('');
   122→      setAddRepBl(null);
   123→    } catch {}
   124→  };
   125→
   126→  const toggleBl = (code: string) => {
   127→    setCollapsedBl(prev => {
   128→      const next = new Set(prev);
   129→      if (next.has(code)) next.delete(code); else next.add(code);
   130→      return next;
   131→    });
   132→  };
   133→
   134→  const parseRapport = (raw: string) => {
   135→    const match = raw.match(/^(\d)\/(\d)(.*)$/);
   136→    if (!match) return { readability: null, strength: null, db_over_s9: null };
   137→    return {
   138→      readability: parseInt(match[1]),
   139→      strength: parseInt(match[2]),
   140→      db_over_s9: match[3]?.trim() || null,
   141→    };
   142→  };
   143→
   144→  const handleBezirkSubmit = async (bezirkCode: string, repeaterId: number, form: EntryForm) => {
   145→    try {
   146→      if (!repeaterId) return;
   147→      const operator = await getOrCreateOperator(form.callsign, form.operator);
   148→      if (!operator) return;
   149→
   150→      const parsed = parseRapport(form.rapport);
   151→
   152→      try {
   153→        const report = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {
   154→          method: 'POST',
   155→          body: JSON.stringify({
   156→            operator_id: operator.id,
   157→            repeater_id: repeaterId,
   158→            ...parsed,
   159→            notes: form.notes || null,
   160→            bezirk_code: (bezirkCode && bezirkCode !== '??') ? bezirkCode : null,
   161→          }),
   162→        });
   163→        onReportCreated(report);
   164→      } catch (err: any) {
   165→        if (err.message?.includes('existiert') && err.data?.existing_report) {
   166→          const existing = err.data.existing_report;
   167→          const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${existing.id}`, {
   168→            method: 'PATCH',
   169→            body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: form.notes || null, bezirk_code: (bezirkCode && bezirkCode !== '??') ? bezirkCode : null }),
   170→          });
   171→          onReportUpdated(updated);
   172→        }
   173→      }
   174→    } catch {
   175→    }
   176→  };
   177→
   178→  const handleEdit = (report: any) => {
   179→    setEditingId(report.id);
   180→    setEditForm({
   181→      callsign: report.callsign,
   182→      operator: { id: report.operator_id, callsign: report.callsign, name: report.operator_name || null, qth: report.operator_qth || null },
   183→      rapport: report.readability && report.strength ? `${report.readability}/${report.strength}${report.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '',
   184→      notes: report.notes || '',
   185→      opName: report.operator_name || '',
   186→      opQth: report.operator_qth || '',
   187→      bezirkCode: report.bezirk_code || '',
   188→    });
   189→  };
   190→
   191→  const handleUpdate = async () => {
   192→    if (!editingId) return;
   193→    const parsed = parseRapport(editForm.rapport);
   194→    try {
   195→      // Save operator name/qth if changed
   196→      if (editForm.operator?.id) {
   197→        const nameChanged = (editForm.opName || '') !== (editForm.operator.name || '');
   198→        const qthChanged = (editForm.opQth || '') !== (editForm.operator.qth || '');
   199→        if (nameChanged || qthChanged) {
   200→          const patch: any = {};
   201→          if (nameChanged) patch.name = editForm.opName || null;
   202→          if (qthChanged) patch.qth = editForm.opQth || null;
   203→          await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${editForm.operator.id}`, {
   204→            method: 'PATCH',
   205→            body: JSON.stringify(patch),
   206→          });
   207→        }
   208→      }
   209→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${editingId}`, {
   210→        method: 'PATCH',
   211→        body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: editForm.notes || null, bezirk_code: editForm.bezirkCode || null }),
   212→      });
   213→      if (editForm.opName !== undefined) updated.operator_name = editForm.opName || null;
   214→      if (editForm.opQth !== undefined) updated.operator_qth = editForm.opQth || null;
   215→      onReportUpdated(updated);
   216→      setEditingId(null);
   217→    } catch {}
   218→  };
   219→
   220→  const handleMoveToBezirk = async (reportId: number, newBezirkCode: string | null) => {
   221→    try {
   222→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, {
   223→        method: 'PATCH',
   224→        body: JSON.stringify({ bezirk_code: newBezirkCode }),
   225→      });
   226→      onReportUpdated(updated);
   227→    } catch {}
   228→  };
   229→
   230→  const handleDelete = async (reportId: number) => {
   231→    if (!confirm('Rapport löschen?')) return;
   232→    try {
   233→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
   234→      onReportDeleted(reportId);
   235→    } catch {}
   236→  };
   237→
   238→  const domesticBlCodes = new Set(bundeslaender.filter(b => !b.is_international).map(b => b.code));
   239→
   240→  return (
   241→    <div className="space-y-2">
   242→      {bundeslaender.filter(bl => !bl.is_international).map(bl => {
   243→        const blBezirke = bezirke.filter(b => b.bundesland_code === bl.code);
   244→        const defaultRepId = blRepSelection[bl.code] || 0;
   245→        // Match reports by bezirk's Bundesland, only for linked repeaters
   246→        const blBezirkCodes = new Set(blBezirke.map(b => b.code));
   247→        const blReports = reports.filter(r => {
   248→          if (!r.repeater_is_linked) return false;
   249→          // Primary: match by bezirk code
   250→          if (r.bezirk_code && blBezirkCodes.has(r.bezirk_code)) return true;
   251→          // Fallback for null bezirk_code: use operator BL (if domestic), else repeater BL
   252→          if (!r.bezirk_code) {
   253→            const effectiveBl = domesticBlCodes.has(r.bundesland_code) ? r.bundesland_code : r.repeater_bundesland_code;
   254→            return effectiveBl === bl.code;
   255→          }
   256→          return false;
   257→        });
   258→        const isCollapsed = collapsedBl.has(bl.code);
   259→        const reportCount = blReports.filter(r => !r.is_op_marker && r.readability).length;
   260→
   261→        return (
   262→          <div key={bl.code} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm">
   263→            <div className="bg-gray-50 px-2 sm:px-3 py-2 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between border-b gap-1">
   264→              <div
   265→                onClick={() => toggleBl(bl.code)}
   266→                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100 rounded px-1 -mx-1 min-w-0"
   267→              >
   268→                <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">{isCollapsed ? '▶' : '▼'}</span>
   269→                <span className="font-medium text-xs sm:text-sm truncate">OE{parseInt(bl.code)} {bl.name}</span>
   270→                <span className="bg-[#0d6efd] text-white text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full flex-shrink-0">{reportCount}</span>
   271→              </div>
   272→              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
   273→                <button
   274→                  onClick={() => setAddRepBl(addRepBl === bl.code ? null : bl.code)}
   275→                  className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 hidden sm:inline"
   276→                >
   277→                  + Umsetzer
   278→                </button>
   279→                <label className={`text-xs ${blOpCallsigns[bl.code] ? 'text-gray-500' : 'text-[#c8102e] font-semibold'}`}>OP:</label>
   280→                <input
   281→                  type="text"
   282→                  value={blOpCallsigns[bl.code] || ''}
   283→                  onChange={e => setBlOpCallsigns(prev => ({ ...prev, [bl.code]: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   284→                  onBlur={e => saveOpCallsign(bl.code, e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
   285→                  placeholder="Rufz."
   286→                  className={`border rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono uppercase w-20 sm:w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 ${blOpCallsigns[bl.code] ? 'border-gray-300' : 'border-[#c8102e] placeholder-[#c8102e]/60'}`}
   287→                />
   288→              </div>
   289→            </div>
   290→
   291→            {!isCollapsed && (
   292→              <div>
   293→                {addRepBl === bl.code && (
   294→                  <div className="px-3 py-2 bg-blue-50 border-b flex items-center gap-1 flex-wrap">
   295→                    <input
   296→                      type="text"
   297→                      value={newRepName}
   298→                      onChange={e => setNewRepName(e.target.value)}
   299→                      placeholder="Name"
   300→                      className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-28 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   301→                      autoFocus
   302→                    />
   303→                    <input
   304→                      type="text"
   305→                      value={newRepFreq}
   306→                      onChange={e => setNewRepFreq(e.target.value)}
   307→                      placeholder="MHz"
   308→                      className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   309→                    />
   310→                    <input
   311→                      type="text"
   312→                      value={newRepCallsign}
   313→                      onChange={e => setNewRepCallsign(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
   314→                      placeholder="Rufz."
   315→                      className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono uppercase w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   316→                    />
   317→                    <button
   318→                      onClick={() => addRepeater(bl.code)}
   319→                      className="bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs"
   320→                    >
   321→                      OK
   322→                    </button>
   323→                    <button
   324→                      onClick={() => { setAddRepBl(null); setNewRepName(''); setNewRepFreq(''); setNewRepCallsign(''); }}
   325→                      className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 text-xs"
   326→                    >
   327→                      Abbrechen
   328→                    </button>
   329→                  </div>
   330→                )}
   331→                <div className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   332→                  {blBezirke.map(bz => {
   333→                    const bzReports = blReports.filter(r => r.bezirk_code === bz.code);
   334→                    return (
   335→                      <BezirkRow
   336→                        key={bz.code}
   337→                        bezirk={bz}
   338→                        reports={bzReports}
   339→                        availableBezirke={blBezirke}
   340→                        linkedRepeaters={linkedRepeaters}
   341→                        defaultRepeaterId={defaultRepId}
   342→                        onSubmit={(repeaterId, form) => handleBezirkSubmit(bz.code, repeaterId, form)}
   343→                        onEdit={handleEdit}
   344→                        onDelete={handleDelete}
   345→                        onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   346→                        editingId={editingId}
   347→                        editForm={editForm}
   348→                        onEditChange={setEditForm}
   349→                        onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   350→                        onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   351→                        dragReportId={dragReportId}
   352→                        onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   353→                        onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   354→                      />
   355→                    );
   356→                  })}
   357→                  {/* Sonstige — reports without matching bezirk + entry form */}
   358→                  {(() => {
   359→                    const bzCodes = new Set(blBezirke.map(b => b.code));
   360→                    const unassigned = blReports.filter(r => !r.bezirk_code || !bzCodes.has(r.bezirk_code));
   361→                    return (
   362→                      <BezirkRow
   363→                        bezirk={{ code: '??', name: 'Sonstige', is_capital: false }}

   366→                        linkedRepeaters={linkedRepeaters}
   367→                        defaultRepeaterId={defaultRepId}
   368→                        onSubmit={(repeaterId, form) => handleBezirkSubmit('??', repeaterId, form)}
   369→                        onEdit={handleEdit}

   392→
   393→interface BezirkRowProps {
   394→  bezirk: any;
   395→  reports: any[];
   396→  availableBezirke: any[];
   397→  linkedRepeaters: any[];
   398→  defaultRepeaterId: number;
   399→  onSubmit: (repeaterId: number, form: EntryForm) => void;
   400→  onEdit: (report: any) => void;
   401→  onDelete: (id: number) => void;
   402→  onMoveToBezirk: (reportId: number, bezirkCode: string | null) => void;
   403→  editingId: number | null;
   404→  editForm: EntryForm;
   405→  onEditChange: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   406→  onEditSave: () => void;
   407→  onEditCancel: () => void;
   408→  dragReportId: number | null;
   409→  onDragStart: (reportId: number) => void;
   410→  onDragEnd: () => void;
   411→}
   412→
   413→function BezirkRow({ bezirk, reports, availableBezirke, linkedRepeaters, defaultRepeaterId, onSubmit, onEdit, onDelete, onMoveToBezirk, editingId, editForm, onEditChange, onEditSave, onEditCancel, dragReportId, onDragStart, onDragEnd }: BezirkRowProps) {
   414→  const [form, setForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '' });
   415→  const [repeaterId, setRepeaterId] = useState(defaultRepeaterId);
   416→  const [dragOver, setDragOver] = useState(false);
   417→  const callsignRef = useRef<CallsignInputRef>(null);
   418→  const rapportRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   419→
   420→  useEffect(() => {
   421→    if (defaultRepeaterId && !repeaterId) setRepeaterId(defaultRepeaterId);
   422→  }, [defaultRepeaterId]);
   423→
   424→  const handleSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
   425→    e.preventDefault();
   426→    if (!form.callsign && !form.operator) return;
   427→    if (!repeaterId) return;
   428→    onSubmit(repeaterId, form);
   429→    setForm({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '' });
   430→    setTimeout(() => callsignRef.current?.focus(), 50);
   431→  };
   432→
   433→  const handleDrop = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
   434→    e.preventDefault();
   435→    setDragOver(false);
   436→    if (dragReportId) {
   437→      const targetCode = bezirk.code === '??' ? null : bezirk.code;
   438→      onMoveToBezirk(dragReportId, targetCode);
   439→    }
   440→  };
   441→
   442→  const saveOperatorField = (field: 'name' | 'qth', value: string) => {
   443→    if (!form.operator?.id) return;
   444→    const original = field === 'name' ? (form.operator.name || '') : (form.operator.qth || '');
   445→    if (value === original) return;
   446→    apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${form.operator.id}`, {
   447→      method: 'PATCH',
   448→      body: JSON.stringify({ [field]: value || null }),
   449→    }).then(() => {
   450→      setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: { ...f.operator, [field]: value || null } }));
   451→    }).catch(() => {});
   452→  };
   453→
   454→  return (
   455→    <div
   456→      className={`px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors ${dragOver && dragReportId ? 'bg-blue-100 ring-2 ring-blue-400 ring-inset' : ''}`}
   457→      onDragOver={e => { if (dragReportId) { e.preventDefault(); setDragOver(true); } }}
   458→      onDragLeave={() => setDragOver(false)}
   459→      onDrop={handleDrop}
   460→    >
   461→      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 mb-1">
   462→        <span className={`text-xs font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-white ${bezirk.code === '??' ? 'bg-[#ffc107] text-gray-800' : bezirk.is_capital ? 'bg-[#dc3545]' : 'bg-[#6c757d]'}`}>
   463→          {bezirk.code === '??' ? '??' : bezirk.code}
   464→        </span>
   465→        <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 truncate">{bezirk.name}</span>
   466→        {reports.length > 0 && <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0">({reports.length})</span>}
   467→      </div>
   468→
   469→      {reports.length > 0 && (
   470→        <table className="w-full text-xs mb-1">
   471→          <tbody>
   472→            {reports.map((r, idx) => (
   473→              <tr
   474→                key={r.id}
   475→                className={`hover:bg-blue-50 group ${dragReportId === r.id ? 'opacity-40' : ''}`}
   476→                draggable={editingId !== r.id}
   477→                onDragStart={e => { e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move'; onDragStart(r.id); }}
   478→                onDragEnd={onDragEnd}
   479→              >
   480→                {editingId === r.id ? (
   481→                  <td colSpan={6} className="py-0.5">
   482→                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-blue-50 rounded p-1 flex-wrap" onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); onEditSave(); } }}>
   483→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}</span>
   484→                      <input value={editForm.opName} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opName: e.target.value })} placeholder="Name" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 hidden sm:block" />
   485→                      <input value={editForm.opQth} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opQth: e.target.value })} placeholder="QTH" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-14 sm:w-16 hidden sm:block" />
   486→                      <input value={editForm.rapport} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" autoFocus />
   487→                      <input value={editForm.notes} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, notes: e.target.value })} placeholder="Sonst." className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" />
   488→                      <select
   489→                        value={editForm.bezirkCode}
   490→                        onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, bezirkCode: e.target.value })}
   491→                        className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-14 sm:w-16"
   492→                        title="Bezirk"
   493→                      >
   494→                        <option value="">??</option>
   495→                        {availableBezirke.map(bz => <option key={bz.code} value={bz.code}>{bz.code}</option>)}
   496→                      </select>
   497→                      <button onClick={onEditSave} className="text-green-600 text-xs font-bold">OK</button>
   498→                      <button onClick={onEditCancel} className="text-gray-400 text-xs">X</button>
   499→                    </div>
   500→                  </td>
   501→                ) : (
   502→                  <>
   503→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-400 w-4 cursor-grab">{idx + 1}</td>
   504→                    <td className="py-0.5 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   505→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}</span>
   506→                      {r.operator_name && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-500 text-xs">{r.operator_name}</span>}
   507→                      {r.operator_qth && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-400 text-xs hidden sm:inline">{r.operator_qth}</span>}
   508→                    </td>
   509→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-blue-600 text-xs cursor-pointer hidden sm:table-cell" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>{r.repeater_name || ''}</td>
   510→                    <td className="py-0.5 font-mono text-gray-500 w-16 sm:w-24 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   511→                      {r.readability && r.strength ? `${r.readability}/${r.strength}${r.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '—'}
   512→                    </td>
   513→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-500 cursor-pointer hidden sm:table-cell" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>{r.notes || ''}</td>
   514→                    <td className="py-0.5 w-4">
   515→                      <button onClick={() => onDelete(r.id)} className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100">x</button>
   516→                    </td>
   517→                  </>
   518→                )}
   519→              </tr>
   520→            ))}
   521→          </tbody>
   522→        </table>
   523→      )}
   524→
   525→      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex items-center gap-1">
   526→        {linkedRepeaters.length > 0 && (
   527→          <select
   528→            value={repeaterId || ''}
   529→            onChange={e => setRepeaterId(parseInt(e.target.value))}
   530→            className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs max-w-[80px] sm:max-w-none focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   531→          >
   532→            {linkedRepeaters.map(r => (
   533→              <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>
   534→                {r.site_name || r.short_name}{r.callsign ? ` (${r.callsign})` : ''}
   535→              </option>
   536→            ))}
   537→          </select>
   538→        )}
   539→        <CallsignInput
   540→          ref={callsignRef}
   541→          value={form.callsign}
   542→          onChange={v => setForm(f => ({ ...f, callsign: v, operator: null, opName: '', opQth: '' }))}
   543→          onSelect={op => {
   544→            setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: op, callsign: op.callsign, opName: op.name || '', opQth: op.qth || '' }));
   545→            setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50);
   546→          }}
   547→          onEnter={() => setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50)}
   548→          className="w-24 sm:w-28"
   549→        />
   550→        <input
   551→          type="text"
   552→          value={form.opName}
   553→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opName: e.target.value }))}
   554→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('name', e.target.value)}
   555→          placeholder="Name"
   556→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-20 sm:w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   557→        />
   558→        <input
   559→          type="text"
   560→          value={form.opQth}
   561→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opQth: e.target.value }))}
   562→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('qth', e.target.value)}
   563→          placeholder="QTH"
   564→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   565→        />
   566→        <input
   567→          ref={rapportRef}
   568→          type="text"
   569→          value={form.rapport}
   570→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   571→          placeholder="5/9"
   572→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   573→          onKeyDown={e => {
   574→            if (e.altKey && e.key >= '1' && e.key <= '9') {
   575→              e.preventDefault();
   576→              setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: `5/${e.key}` }));
   577→            }
   578→          }}
   579→        />
   580→        <input
   581→          type="text"
   582→          value={form.notes}
   583→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, notes: e.target.value }))}
   584→          placeholder="Sonst."
   585→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs flex-1 min-w-[40px] sm:min-w-[60px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   586→        />
   587→        <button type="submit" title="Rapport speichern (Enter)" className="bg-[#c8102e] hover:bg-[#a00d24] text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs flex-shrink-0">
   588→          + ↵
   589→        </button>
   590→      </form>
   591→    </div>
   592→  );
   593→}

   206→  }
   207→
   208→  const avgParticipants = exercises.length > 0
   209→    ? Math.round((stats?.totalParticipants ?? 0) / exercises.length * 10) / 10
   210→    : 0;
   211→
   212→  return (
   213→    <div ref={pageRef} className="space-y-6">
   214→      {/* Header */}
   215→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
   216→        <div className="min-w-0">
   217→          <h1 className="text-base sm:text-xl font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">Auswertung</h1>
   218→          {exercises.length > 0 && (
   219→            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-500">
   220→              {formatDateDE(from)} – {formatDateDE(to)} · {exercises.length} Übung{exercises.length !== 1 ? 'en' : ''}
   221→            </p>
   222→          )}
   223→        </div>
   224→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-shrink-0" data-no-export="true">
   225→          {stats && exercises.length > 0 && (
   226→            <>
   227→              <button
   228→                onClick={async () => {
   229→                  try {
   230→                    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
   231→                    const res = await fetch(`/api/v1/reports/adif?from=${from}&to=${to}`, {
   232→                      headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {},
   233→                    });
   234→                    if (!res.ok) throw new Error();
   235→                    const blob = await res.blob();
   236→                    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
   237→                    const link = document.createElement('a');
   238→                    link.href = url;
   239→                    link.download = `BOS-ARSA_QSOs_${from}_${to}.adi`;
   240→                    link.click();
   241→                    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
   242→                  } catch {}
   243→                }}
   244→                className="bg-red-700 hover:bg-red-800 text-white px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs sm:text-sm"
   245→                title="ADIF-Datei für QRZ.com, LOTW etc."
   246→              >
   247→                Download ADIF
   248→              </button>
   249→              <button
   250→                onClick={downloadFullPage}
   251→                disabled={exporting}
   252→                className="bg-[#1e3a5f] hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] text-white px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs sm:text-sm disabled:opacity-50"
   253→              >
   254→                {exporting ? '...' : 'Download Auswertung'}
   255→              </button>
   256→            </>
   257→          )}
   258→        </div>
   259→      </div>
   260→
   261→      {/* Date range selector */}
   262→      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-3 sm:p-4" data-no-export="true">
   263→        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-3">
   264→          {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((q) => (
   265→            <button
   266→              key={q}
   267→              onClick={() => applyQuarter(q)}
   268→              className="bg-gray-100 hover:bg-blue-100 text-gray-700 hover:text-[#1e3a5f] px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   269→            >
   270→              Q{q}
   271→            </button>
   272→          ))}
   273→          <button
   274→            onClick={applyYear}
   275→            className="bg-gray-100 hover:bg-blue-100 text-gray-700 hover:text-[#1e3a5f] px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   276→          >
   277→            Jahr {year}
   278→          </button>
   279→        </div>
   280→        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm">
   281→          <label className="text-gray-500 text-xs">Von</label>
   282→          <input
   283→            type="date"
   284→            value={from}
   285→            onChange={(e) => handleFromChange(e.target.value)}
   286→            className="border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm"
   287→          />
   288→          <label className="text-gray-500 text-xs">Bis</label>
   289→          <input
   290→            type="date"
   291→            value={to}
   292→            onChange={(e) => handleToChange(e.target.value)}
   293→            className="border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm"
   294→          />
   295→        </div>
   296→        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-gray-100">
   297→          <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">Übungstyp:</span>
   298→          {EXERCISE_TYPES.map(t => (
   299→            <label key={t.value} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer text-sm">
   300→              <input
   301→                type="checkbox"
   302→                checked={selectedTypes.has(t.value)}
   303→                onChange={() => toggleType(t.value)}
   304→                className="w-3.5 h-3.5 accent-red-600"
   305→              />
   306→              <span className={selectedTypes.has(t.value) ? 'text-[#1e3a5f] font-medium' : 'text-gray-600'}>{t.short}</span>
   307→            </label>
   308→          ))}
   309→          {selectedTypes.size > 0 && (
   310→            <button
   311→              onClick={() => { setSelectedTypes(new Set()); fetchStats(from, to, new Set()); }}
   312→              className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600"
   313→            >
   314→              Alle anzeigen
   315→            </button>
   316→          )}
   317→        </div>
   318→      </div>
   319→
   320→      {loading && <p className="text-gray-500">Laden...</p>}
   321→
   322→      {!loading && exercises.length === 0 && (
   323→        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-6 text-center text-gray-500">
   324→          Keine Übungen im gewählten Zeitraum gefunden.
   325→        </div>
   326→      )}
   327→
   328→      {!loading && stats && exercises.length > 0 && (
   329→        <>
   330→          {/* Summary cards */}
   331→          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-2 sm:gap-3">
   332→            <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-2 sm:p-4 text-center">
   333→              <div className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">{stats.totalParticipants}</div>
   334→              <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-500">Teilnehmer (gesamt)</div>
   335→            </div>
   336→            <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-2 sm:p-4 text-center">
   337→              <div className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">{stats.totalReports}</div>
   338→              <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-500">Rapporte</div>
   339→            </div>
   340→            <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-2 sm:p-4 text-center">
   341→              <div className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">{exercises.length}</div>
   342→              <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-500">Übungen</div>
   343→            </div>
   344→            <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-2 sm:p-4 text-center">
   345→              <div className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-gray-700">{avgParticipants}</div>
   346→              <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-500">Ø Teilnehmer/Übung</div>
   347→            </div>
   348→          </div>
   349→
   350→          {/* Per-repeater summary */}
   351→          {stats.perRepeater.length > 0 && (
   352→            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-2">
   353→              {stats.perRepeater.map((r) => (
   354→                <div key={r.short_name} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-2 sm:p-3 text-center">
   355→                  <div className="text-lg sm:text-xl font-bold text-gray-700">{r.count}</div>
   356→                  <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 truncate">{r.short_name}</div>
   357→                </div>
   358→              ))}
   359→            </div>
   360→          )}
   361→
   362→          {/* Collapsible exercise list */}
   363→          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden">
   364→            <div
   365→              onClick={() => setShowExercises((v) => !v)}
   366→              className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold cursor-pointer hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] flex items-center justify-between"
   367→            >
   368→              <span>Übungen ({exercises.length})</span>
   369→              <span className="text-xs">{showExercises ? '\u25BC' : '\u25B6'}</span>
   370→            </div>
   371→            {showExercises && (
   372→              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
   373→                <table className="w-full text-xs sm:text-sm min-w-[360px]">
   374→                  <thead>
   375→                    <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b text-left text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">
   376→                      <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Datum</th>
   377→                      <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 hidden sm:table-cell">Typ</th>
   378→                      <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Name</th>
   379→                      <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-20">Teiln.</th>
   380→                      <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-20">Rapp.</th>
   381→                    </tr>
   382→                  </thead>
   383→                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   384→                    {exercises.map((ex) => (
   385→                      <tr key={ex.id} className="hover:bg-blue-50">
   386→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs">{formatDateDE(ex.date)}</td>
   387→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1.5 text-xs text-gray-500 hidden sm:table-cell">{getShortLabel(ex.exercise_type || '')}</td>
   388→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1.5">{ex.name}</td>
   389→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1.5 text-right">{ex.participant_count}</td>
   390→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1.5 text-right">{ex.report_count}</td>
   391→                      </tr>
   392→                    ))}
   393→                  </tbody>
   394→                </table>
   395→              </div>
   396→            )}
   397→          </div>
   398→
   399→          {/* Austria Map */}
   400→          {stats.blStats.length > 0 && (
   401→            <div ref={mapCardRef} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-3 sm:p-4">
   402→              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 sm:mb-3">
   403→                <h2 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f]">
   404→                  Teilnehmer nach Bundesland / Land
   405→                </h2>
   406→                <button
   407→                  onClick={() => downloadChart(mapCardRef, `BOS-ARSA_Karte_${from}_${to}.png`)}
   408→                  className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 ml-2"
   409→                  title="Karte als PNG herunterladen"
   410→                  data-no-export="true"
   411→                >
   412→                  Download
   413→                </button>
   414→              </div>
   415→              <AustriaMap
   416→                data={stats.blStats.map((bl) => ({
   417→                  code: bl.bundesland_code,
   418→                  participants: bl.participants,
   419→                  reports: bl.reports,
   420→                }))}
   421→              />
   422→            </div>
   423→          )}
   424→
   425→          {/* Charts */}
   426→          {(() => {
   427→            const chartData = stats.bezirkStats.length > 0
   428→              ? stats.bezirkStats.map(bz => ({ label: `OE${parseInt(bz.bundesland_code, 10)} ${bz.bezirk_code}`, participants: bz.participants, reports: bz.reports }))
   429→              : stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: `OE${parseInt(bl.bundesland_code, 10)}`, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));
   430→
   431→            return chartData.length > 0 ? (
   432→              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
   433→                {/* Bar chart */}
   434→                <div ref={barCardRef} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-3 sm:p-4">
   435→                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 sm:mb-3">
   436→                    <h2 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f]">
   437→                      Auswertung {formatDateDE(from)} – {formatDateDE(to)}
   438→                    </h2>
   439→                    <button
   440→                      onClick={() => downloadChart(barCardRef, `BOS-ARSA_Stationen_${from}_${to}.png`)}
   441→                      className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 ml-2"
   442→                      title="Grafik als PNG herunterladen"
   443→                      data-no-export="true"
   444→                    >
   445→                      Download
   446→                    </button>
   447→                  </div>
   448→                  <div className="h-48 sm:h-[350px]">
   449→                    <Bar
   450→                      ref={barChartRef}
   451→                      data={{
   452→                        labels: chartData.map(d => d.label),
   453→                        datasets: [
   454→                          {
   455→                            label: 'Stationen',
   456→                            data: chartData.map(d => d.participants),
   457→                            backgroundColor: '#1e3a5f',
   458→                          },
   459→                          {
   460→                            label: 'Rapporte',
   461→                            data: chartData.map(d => d.reports),
   462→                            backgroundColor: '#c8102e',
   463→                          },
   464→                        ],
   465→                      }}
   466→                      options={{
   467→                        responsive: true,
   468→                        maintainAspectRatio: false,
   469→                        plugins: { legend: { position: 'bottom' } },
   470→                        scales: {
   471→                          x: { ticks: { maxRotation: 90, minRotation: 45, font: { size: 9 } } },
   472→                          y: { beginAtZero: true },
   473→                        },
   474→                      }}
   475→                    />
   476→                  </div>
   477→                </div>
   478→
   479→                {/* Pie chart */}
   480→                <div ref={pieCardRef} className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-3 sm:p-4">
   481→                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 sm:mb-3">
   482→                    <h2 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f]">
   483→                      Rapporte Verteilung nach Bundesland / Land
   484→                    </h2>
   485→                    <button
   486→                      onClick={() => downloadChart(pieCardRef, `BOS-ARSA_Verteilung_${from}_${to}.png`)}
   487→                      className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 ml-2"
   488→                      title="Grafik als PNG herunterladen"
   489→                      data-no-export="true"
   490→                    >
   491→                      Download
   492→                    </button>
   493→                  </div>
   494→                  <div className="h-48 sm:h-[350px] flex items-center justify-center">
   495→                    <Pie
   496→                      ref={pieChartRef}
   497→                      data={{
   498→                        labels: chartData.filter(d => d.reports > 0).map(d => d.label),
   499→                        datasets: [{
   500→                          data: chartData.filter(d => d.reports > 0).map(d => d.reports),
   501→                          backgroundColor: PIE_COLORS,
   502→                        }],
   503→                      }}
   504→                      options={{
   505→                        responsive: true,
   506→                        maintainAspectRatio: false,
   507→                        plugins: {
   508→                          legend: { position: 'right', labels: { font: { size: 10 } } },
   509→                        },
   510→                      }}
   511→                    />
   512→                  </div>
   513→                </div>
   514→              </div>
   515→            ) : null;
   516→          })()}
   517→
   518→          {/* Bundesland table (when no bezirk data) */}
   519→          {stats.bezirkStats.length === 0 && stats.blStats.length > 0 && (
   520→            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden">
   521→              <div className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold">
   522→                Teilnehmer nach Bundesland / Land
   523→              </div>
   524→              <table className="w-full text-sm">
   525→                <thead>
   526→                  <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b text-left text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">
   527→                    <th className="px-4 py-2">Bundesland</th>
   528→                    <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right w-28">Teilnehmer</th>
   529→                    <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right w-28">Rapporte</th>
   530→                  </tr>
   531→                </thead>
   532→                <tbody>
   533→                  {stats.blStats.map(bl => (
   534→                    <tr key={bl.bundesland_code} className="border-t hover:bg-blue-50">
   535→                      <td className="px-4 py-1.5 font-medium">{bl.bundesland}</td>
   536→                      <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right">{bl.participants}</td>
   537→                      <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right">{bl.reports}</td>
   538→                    </tr>
   539→                  ))}
   540→                  <tr className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white font-bold">
   541→                    <td className="px-4 py-2">Gesamt</td>
   542→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalParticipants}</td>
   543→                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalReports}</td>
   544→                  </tr>
   545→                </tbody>
   546→              </table>
   547→            </div>
   548→          )}
   549→
   550→          {/* Bezirk/Bundesland table */}
   551→          {stats.bezirkStats.length > 0 && (
   552→            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden">
   553→              <div className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold">
   554→                Nebenstationen nach Bezirk
   555→              </div>
   556→              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
   557→                <table className="w-full text-xs sm:text-sm min-w-[360px]">
   558→                  <thead>
   559→                    <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b text-left text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">
   560→                      <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Bundesland / Bezirk</th>
   561→                      <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-16 sm:w-28">Teiln.</th>
   562→                      <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-16 sm:w-28">Rapp.</th>
   563→                    </tr>
   564→                  </thead>
   565→                  <tbody>
   566→                    {Object.entries(byBl).map(([blCode, bl]) => (
   567→                      <>
   568→                        <tr key={`bl-${blCode}`} className="bg-gray-50 font-medium border-t">
   569→                          <td className="px-4 py-1.5">{bl.name}</td>
   570→                          <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right font-bold">{bl.totalParticipants}</td>
   571→                          <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right font-bold">{bl.totalReports}</td>
   572→                        </tr>
   573→                        {bl.bezirke.map(bz => (
   574→                          <tr key={bz.bezirk_code} className="border-t border-gray-100 hover:bg-blue-50">
   575→                            <td className="px-4 py-1 pl-8">
   576→                              <span className={`inline-block text-xs font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-white mr-2 ${bz.is_capital ? 'bg-[#dc3545]' : 'bg-[#6c757d]'}`}>
   577→                                {bz.bezirk_code}
   578→                              </span>
   579→                              <span className="text-gray-600">{bz.bezirk_name}</span>
   580→                            </td>
   581→                            <td className="px-4 py-1 text-right">{bz.participants}</td>
   582→                            <td className="px-4 py-1 text-right">{bz.reports}</td>
   583→                          </tr>
   584→                        ))}
   585→                      </>
   586→                    ))}
   587→                    <tr className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white font-bold">
   588→                      <td className="px-4 py-2">Gesamt</td>
   589→                      <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalParticipants}</td>
   590→                      <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{stats.totalReports}</td>
   591→                    </tr>
   592→                  </tbody>
   593→                </table>
   594→              </div>
   595→            </div>
   596→          )}
   597→
   598→          {/* Participants list */}
   599→          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden">
   600→            <div
   601→              onClick={() => setShowParticipants(v => !v)}
   602→              className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold cursor-pointer hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] flex items-center justify-between"
   603→            >
   604→              <span>Teilnehmer-Liste ({stats.participants.length})</span>
   605→              <span className="text-xs">{showParticipants ? '\u25BC' : '\u25B6'}</span>
   606→            </div>
   607→            {showParticipants && (
   608→              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
   609→                <table className="w-full text-xs sm:text-sm min-w-[360px]">
   610→                  <thead>
   611→                    <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b text-left text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase">
   612→                      <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 w-8">#</th>
   613→                      <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Rufzeichen</th>
   614→                      <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 hidden sm:table-cell">Name</th>
   615→                      <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2">Bezirk</th>
   616→                      <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 hidden md:table-cell">Bundesland / Land</th>
   617→                      <th className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 text-right w-16 sm:w-24">Rapp.</th>
   618→                    </tr>
   619→                  </thead>
   620→                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   621→                    {stats.participants.map((p, idx) => (
   622→                      <tr key={p.callsign} className="hover:bg-blue-50">
   623→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-xs text-gray-400">{idx + 1}</td>
   624→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 font-mono font-medium">{p.callsign}</td>
   625→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-gray-600 hidden sm:table-cell">{p.name || ''}</td>
   626→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1">
   627→                          {p.bezirk_code && (
   628→                            <span className="text-xs bg-gray-200 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono">{p.bezirk_code}</span>
   629→                          )}
   630→                        </td>
   631→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-gray-600 text-xs hidden md:table-cell">{p.bundesland_name || ''}</td>
   632→                        <td className="px-2 sm:px-4 py-1 text-right font-mono">{p.report_count}</td>
   633→                      </tr>
   634→                    ))}
   635→                  </tbody>
   636→                </table>
   637→              </div>
   638→            )}
   639→          </div>
   640→
   641→          {/* Export section */}
   642→          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4" data-no-export="true">
   643→            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#1e3a5f] mb-3">Export</h2>
   644→            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
   645→              <button
   646→                onClick={downloadFullPage}
   647→                disabled={exporting}
   648→                className="bg-[#1e3a5f] hover:bg-[#2a4a7f] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium disabled:opacity-50"
   649→              >
   650→                {exporting ? 'Exportieren...' : 'Download Auswertung'}
   651→              </button>
   652→              <button
   653→                onClick={async () => {
   654→                  try {
   655→                    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
   656→                    const res = await fetch(`/api/v1/reports/adif?from=${from}&to=${to}`, {
   657→                      headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {},
   658→                    });
   659→                    if (!res.ok) throw new Error();
   660→                    const blob = await res.blob();
   661→                    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
   662→                    const link = document.createElement('a');
   663→                    link.href = url;
   664→                    link.download = `BOS-ARSA_QSOs_${from}_${to}.adi`;
   665→                    link.click();
   666→                    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
   667→                  } catch {}
   668→                }}
   669→                className="bg-red-700 hover:bg-red-800 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   670→              >
   671→                Download ADIF
   672→              </button>
   673→            </div>
   674→            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-2">
   675→              ADIF-Datei kann in QRZ.com, LOTW oder andere Logbuch-Software importiert werden.
   676→            </p>
   677→          </div>
   678→        </>
   679→      )}
   680→    </div>
   681→  );
   682→}
   683→
