    82→    d: 'M550.84,84.85L551.18,84.93L551.18,84.93L551.24,84.74L551.24,84.74L553.13,84.46L553.13,84.46L554.28,86.01L554.28,86.01L554.41,86.46L554.41,86.46L554.55,87.92L557.14,89.89L557.58,87.83L558.77,87.91L560.11,88.61L559.72,90.15L560.84,91.36L562.81,91.53L561.98,101.14L563.03,101.53L563.26,103.45L565.04,103.68L565.32,105.77L565.32,105.77L564.65,106.48L564.65,106.48L560.19,103.85L553.72,106.47L553.97,108.53L545.36,106.43L544.1,108.53L540.76,106.87L536.85,108.01L538.09,106.6L536.79,106.67L535.86,105.13L536.57,104.42L535,104.28L533.75,102.39L535.26,98.75L534.96,96.26L533.71,96.06L534.81,95.39L535.53,91.49L536.19,91.86L536.19,91.86L538.17,94.06L538.17,94.06L539.58,93.98L542.22,90.47L545.7,88.94L545.7,88.94L546.89,88.2L547.42,88.95L547.42,88.95L549.17,88.44L549.4,86.68L549.4,86.68L548.97,85.27L548.97,85.27L550.58,84.72L550.58,84.72z',
    83→    labelX: 550, labelY: 97,
    84→  },
    85→};
    86→
    87→const BL_SHORT: Record<string, string> = {
    88→  '01': 'OE1', '02': 'OE2', '03': 'OE3', '04': 'OE4', '05': 'OE5',
    89→  '06': 'OE6', '07': 'OE7', '08': 'OE8', '09': 'OE9',
    90→};
    91→
    92→// ── Helper functions ─────────────────────────────────────
    93→
    94→function getHeatColor(value: number, max: number): string {
    95→  if (max === 0 || value === 0) return '#e5e7eb';
    96→  const t = value / max;
    97→  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);
    98→  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);
    99→  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);
   100→  return '#' + [r, g, b].map(c => c.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
   101→}
   102→
   103→/** Return white for dark backgrounds, navy for light backgrounds */
   104→function getLabelColor(hexBg: string): string {
   105→  const r = parseInt(hexBg.slice(1, 3), 16);
   106→  const g = parseInt(hexBg.slice(3, 5), 16);
   107→  const b = parseInt(hexBg.slice(5, 7), 16);
   108→  // Relative luminance (perceived brightness)
   109→  const lum = (0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b);
   110→  return lum < 150 ? WHITE : NAVY;
   111→}
   112→
   113→function formatDateDE(d: string): string {
   114→  const date = new Date(d + 'T00:00:00');
   115→  return date.toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
   116→}
   117→
   118→function ensureSpace(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, needed: number) {
   119→  const bottom = doc.page.height - doc.page.margins.bottom;
   120→  if (doc.y + needed > bottom) {
   121→    doc.addPage();
   122→  }
   123→}
   124→
   125→/** Write text at fixed position without moving doc.y */
   126→function textAt(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, str: string, x: number, y: number, opts?: PDFKit.Mixins.TextOptions) {
   127→  doc.text(str, x, y, { lineBreak: false, ...opts });
   128→}
   129→
   130→// ── Render functions ─────────────────────────────────────
   131→
   132→function renderHeader(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, title: string, subtitle: string) {
   133→  const pageW = doc.page.width;
   134→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   135→  const barW = pageW - m.left - m.right;
   136→
   137→  doc.save();
   138→  doc.rect(m.left, m.top, barW, 50).fill(NAVY);
   139→  doc.fontSize(18).fillColor(WHITE);
   140→  textAt(doc, 'BOS-ARSA Log', m.left + 15, m.top + 10);
   141→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor('#93c5fd');
   142→  textAt(doc, 'Im Sinne der Sicherheit', m.left + 15, m.top + 32);
   143→  doc.restore();
   144→
   145→  doc.y = m.top + 60;
   146→  doc.fontSize(14).fillColor(NAVY);
   147→  textAt(doc, title, m.left, doc.y);
   148→  doc.y += 18;
   149→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   150→  textAt(doc, subtitle, m.left, doc.y);
   151→  doc.y += 16;
   152→}
   153→
   154→function renderSummaryCards(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, stats: PdfStats, exerciseCount?: number) {
   155→  ensureSpace(doc, 50);
   156→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   157→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   158→  const cols = exerciseCount !== undefined ? 4 : 2;
   159→  const cardW = usable / cols - 8;
   160→  const startY = doc.y;
   161→
   162→  const cards: { value: string | number; label: string }[] = [
   163→    { value: stats.totalParticipants, label: 'Teilnehmer' },
   164→    { value: stats.totalReports, label: 'Rapporte' },
   165→  ];
   166→  if (exerciseCount !== undefined) {
   167→    cards.push({ value: exerciseCount, label: 'Übungen' });
   168→    const avg = exerciseCount > 0 ? Math.round(stats.totalParticipants / exerciseCount * 10) / 10 : 0;
   169→    cards.push({ value: avg, label: 'Ø Teiln./Übung' });
   170→  }
   171→
   172→  cards.forEach((c, i) => {
   173→    const x = m.left + i * (cardW + 8);
   174→    doc.save();
   175→    doc.roundedRect(x, startY, cardW, 40, 4).fill(GRAY_50);
   176→    doc.fontSize(18).fillColor(NAVY);
   177→    textAt(doc, String(c.value), x, startY + 5, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   178→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   179→    textAt(doc, c.label, x, startY + 27, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   180→    doc.restore();
   181→  });
   182→  doc.y = startY + 48;
   183→}
   184→
   185→function renderPerRepeater(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, perRepeater: PerRepeater[]) {
   186→  if (perRepeater.length === 0) return;
   187→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   188→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   189→  const gap = 6;
   190→  const maxCols = 6;
   191→  const cols = Math.min(perRepeater.length, maxCols);
   192→  const cardW = usable / cols - gap;
   193→  const rowH = 28;
   194→  const rows = Math.ceil(perRepeater.length / maxCols);
   195→  ensureSpace(doc, rows * (rowH + gap) + 4);
   196→
   197→  const startY = doc.y;
   198→  perRepeater.forEach((r, i) => {
   199→    const col = i % maxCols;
   200→    const row = Math.floor(i / maxCols);
   201→    const x = m.left + col * (cardW + gap);
   202→    const y = startY + row * (rowH + gap);
   203→    doc.save();
   204→    doc.roundedRect(x, y, cardW, rowH, 3).fill(GRAY_50);
   205→    doc.fontSize(12).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   206→    textAt(doc, String(r.count), x, y + 2, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   207→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   208→    textAt(doc, r.short_name, x, y + 17, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   209→    doc.restore();
   210→  });
   211→  doc.y = startY + rows * (rowH + gap) + 4;
   212→}
   213→
   214→function renderExerciseTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, exercises: ExerciseSummary[]) {
   215→  if (exercises.length === 0) return;
   216→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   217→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   218→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   219→
   220→  // Section header
   221→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   222→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   223→  textAt(doc, `Übungen (${exercises.length})`, m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   224→  doc.y += 20;
   225→
   226→  // Column positions
   227→  const colDate = m.left + 5;
   228→  const colName = m.left + 80;
   229→  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;
   230→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;
   231→
   232→  // Column headers
   233→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   234→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   235→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   236→  textAt(doc, 'DATUM', colDate, hy);
   237→  textAt(doc, 'NAME', colName, hy);
   238→  textAt(doc, 'TEILN.', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   239→  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   240→  doc.y += 16;
   241→
   242→  exercises.forEach((ex, i) => {
   243→    ensureSpace(doc, 14);
   244→    const rowY = doc.y;
   245→    if (i % 2 === 0) {
   246→      doc.save();
   247→      doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 12).fill(GRAY_50);
   248→      doc.restore();
   249→    }
   250→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   251→    textAt(doc, formatDateDE(ex.date), colDate, rowY + 2);
   252→    textAt(doc, ex.name || '', colName, rowY + 2, { width: colPart - colName - 10 });
   253→    textAt(doc, String(ex.participant_count), colPart, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   254→    textAt(doc, String(ex.report_count), colRap, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   255→    doc.y = rowY + 13;
   256→  });
   257→  doc.y += 4;
   258→}
   259→
   260→function renderAustriaMap(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, blStats: BlStat[]) {
   261→  if (blStats.length === 0) return;
   262→  ensureSpace(doc, 200);
   263→
   264→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   265→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   266→  const mapW = Math.min(usable, 400);
   267→  const mapH = mapW * 0.5;
   268→  const offsetX = m.left + (usable - mapW) / 2;
   269→
   270→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);
   271→  textAt(doc, 'Teilnehmer nach Bundesland', m.left, doc.y);
   272→  doc.y += 14;
   273→  const mapStartY = doc.y;
   274→
   275→  const dataMap = new Map(blStats.map(bl => [bl.bundesland_code, bl]));
   276→  const maxP = Math.max(...blStats.map(bl => bl.participants), 1);
   277→
   278→  const svgW = 620;
   279→  const svgH = 300;
   280→  const scale = Math.min(mapW / svgW, mapH / svgH);
   281→
   282→  for (const [code, pathData] of Object.entries(BL_PATHS)) {
   283→    const bl = dataMap.get(code);
   284→    const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;
   285→    const fillColor = getHeatColor(participants, maxP);
   286→
   287→    doc.save();
   288→    doc.translate(offsetX, mapStartY);
   289→    doc.scale(scale);
   290→    doc.path(pathData.d).lineWidth(2 / scale).fillAndStroke(fillColor, WHITE);
   291→    doc.restore();
   292→
   293→    // Label — contrast-aware color based on heatmap background
   294→    const lx = offsetX + pathData.labelX * scale;
   295→    const ly = mapStartY + pathData.labelY * scale;
   296→    const labelColor = getLabelColor(fillColor);
   297→    doc.save();
   298→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(labelColor);
   299→    textAt(doc, BL_SHORT[code] || code, lx - 15, ly - 6, { width: 30, align: 'center' });
   300→    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(labelColor);
   301→    textAt(doc, String(participants), lx - 15, ly + 1, { width: 30, align: 'center' });
   302→    doc.restore();
   303→  }
   304→
   305→  doc.y = mapStartY + mapH + 10;
   306→}
   307→
   308→function renderBarChart(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, chartData: { label: string; participants: number; reports: number }[]) {
   309→  if (chartData.length === 0) return;
   310→  ensureSpace(doc, 180);
   311→
   312→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   313→  const chartW = Math.min(doc.page.width - m.left - m.right, 500);
   314→  const chartH = 140;
   315→  const startX = m.left;
   316→
   317→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);
   318→  textAt(doc, 'Stationen & Rapporte nach Bundesland', m.left, doc.y);
   319→  doc.y += 15;
   320→  const startY = doc.y;
   321→
   322→  const maxVal = Math.max(...chartData.map(d => Math.max(d.participants, d.reports)), 1);
   323→  const barAreaW = chartW - 30;
   324→  const groupW = barAreaW / chartData.length;
   325→  const barW = Math.min(groupW * 0.35, 20);
   326→
   327→  // Y-axis gridlines
   328→  for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
   329→    const y = startY + chartH - (chartH * i / 4);
   330→    const val = Math.round(maxVal * i / 4);
   331→    doc.save();
   332→    doc.strokeColor(GRAY_200).lineWidth(0.5).moveTo(startX + 25, y).lineTo(startX + chartW, y).stroke();
   333→    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   334→    textAt(doc, String(val), startX, y - 3, { width: 22, align: 'right' });
   335→    doc.restore();
   336→  }
   337→
   338→  // Bars
   339→  chartData.forEach((d, i) => {
   340→    const groupX = startX + 30 + i * groupW;
   341→    const pH = (d.participants / maxVal) * chartH;
   342→    doc.rect(groupX, startY + chartH - pH, barW, pH).fill(NAVY);
   343→    const rH = (d.reports / maxVal) * chartH;
   344→    doc.rect(groupX + barW + 2, startY + chartH - rH, barW, rH).fill(RED);
   345→    doc.save();
   346→    doc.fontSize(4).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   347→    textAt(doc, d.label, groupX - 2, startY + chartH + 3, { width: groupW });
   348→    doc.restore();
   349→  });
   350→
   351→  // Legend
   352→  const legendY = startY + chartH + 14;
   353→  doc.rect(startX + 30, legendY, 8, 6).fill(NAVY);
   354→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   355→  textAt(doc, 'Stationen', startX + 42, legendY);
   356→  doc.rect(startX + 90, legendY, 8, 6).fill(RED);
   357→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   358→  textAt(doc, 'Rapporte', startX + 102, legendY);
   359→  doc.y = legendY + 14;
   360→}
   361→
   362→function renderPieChart(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, chartData: { label: string; value: number }[]) {
   363→  const filtered = chartData.filter(d => d.value > 0);
   364→  if (filtered.length === 0) return;
   365→  ensureSpace(doc, 180);
   366→
   367→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   368→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   369→
   370→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);
   371→  textAt(doc, 'Rapporte Verteilung', m.left, doc.y);
   372→  doc.y += 12;
   373→
   374→  const cx = m.left + 100;
   375→  const cy = doc.y + 70;
   376→  const r = 60;
   377→  const total = filtered.reduce((s, d) => s + d.value, 0);
   378→  let startAngle = -Math.PI / 2;
   379→
   380→  filtered.forEach((d, i) => {
   381→    const sliceAngle = (d.value / total) * 2 * Math.PI;
   382→    const endAngle = startAngle + sliceAngle;
   383→    const color = PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length];
   384→
   385→    const x1 = cx + r * Math.cos(startAngle);
   386→    const y1 = cy + r * Math.sin(startAngle);
   387→    const x2 = cx + r * Math.cos(endAngle);
   388→    const y2 = cy + r * Math.sin(endAngle);
   389→    const largeArc = sliceAngle > Math.PI ? 1 : 0;
   390→
   391→    doc.path(`M ${cx} ${cy} L ${x1} ${y1} A ${r} ${r} 0 ${largeArc} 1 ${x2} ${y2} Z`).fill(color);
   392→    startAngle = endAngle;
   393→  });
   394→
   395→  // Legend (limit to available space)
   396→  const legendX = cx + r + 30;
   397→  const maxLegendItems = Math.floor(((doc.page.height - m.bottom) - (cy - r)) / 12);
   398→  const legendItems = filtered.slice(0, maxLegendItems);
   399→  legendItems.forEach((d, i) => {
   400→    const ly = cy - r + i * 12;
   401→    const color = PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length];
   402→    doc.rect(legendX, ly, 8, 8).fill(color);
   403→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   404→    textAt(doc, `${d.label} (${d.value})`, legendX + 12, ly + 1, { width: usable - legendX + m.left - 12 });
   405→  });
   406→  if (filtered.length > maxLegendItems) {
   407→    const overflowY = cy - r + maxLegendItems * 12;
   408→    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   409→    textAt(doc, `… +${filtered.length - maxLegendItems} weitere`, legendX, overflowY);
   410→  }
   411→
   412→  doc.y = cy + r + 15;
   413→}
   414→
   415→function renderBezirkTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, bezirkStats: BezirkStat[], stats: PdfStats) {
   416→  if (bezirkStats.length === 0) return;
   417→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   418→
   419→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   420→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   421→  const colName = m.left + 5;
   422→  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;
   423→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;
   424→
   425→  // Section header
   426→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   427→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   428→  textAt(doc, 'Nebenstationen nach Bezirk', m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   429→  doc.y += 20;
   430→
   431→  // Column headers
   432→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   433→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   434→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   435→  textAt(doc, 'BUNDESLAND / BEZIRK', colName, hy);
   436→  textAt(doc, 'TEILN.', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   437→  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   438→  doc.y += 16;
   439→
   440→  // Group by Bundesland
   441→  const byBl: Record<string, { name: string; bezirke: BezirkStat[]; totalP: number; totalR: number }> = {};
   442→  for (const bz of bezirkStats) {
   443→    if (!byBl[bz.bundesland_code]) {
   444→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code] = { name: bz.bundesland, bezirke: [], totalP: 0, totalR: 0 };
   445→    }
   446→    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].bezirke.push(bz);
   447→    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalP += bz.participants;
   448→    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalR += bz.reports;
   449→  }
   450→
   451→  for (const [, bl] of Object.entries(byBl)) {
   452→    ensureSpace(doc, 14);
   453→    const rowY = doc.y;
   454→    doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 13).fill(GRAY_100);
   455→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   456→    textAt(doc, bl.name, colName, rowY + 3);
   457→    textAt(doc, String(bl.totalP), colPart, rowY + 3, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   458→    textAt(doc, String(bl.totalR), colRap, rowY + 3, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   459→    doc.y = rowY + 14;
   460→
   461→    bl.bezirke.forEach(bz => {
   462→      ensureSpace(doc, 12);
   463→      const ry = doc.y;
   464→      doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_MED);
   465→      textAt(doc, `    ${bz.bezirk_code}  ${bz.bezirk_name}`, colName, ry + 1);
   466→      textAt(doc, String(bz.participants), colPart, ry + 1, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   467→      textAt(doc, String(bz.reports), colRap, ry + 1, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   468→      doc.y = ry + 11;
   469→    });
   470→  }
   471→
   472→  // Gesamt
   473→  ensureSpace(doc, 18);
   474→  const gy = doc.y;
   475→  doc.rect(m.left, gy, usable, 16).fill(NAVY);
   476→  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(WHITE);
   477→  textAt(doc, 'Gesamt', colName, gy + 4);
   478→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalParticipants), colPart, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   479→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalReports), colRap, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   480→  doc.y = gy + 20;
   481→}
   482→
   483→function renderBlTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, blStats: BlStat[], stats: PdfStats) {
   484→  if (blStats.length === 0) return;
   485→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   486→
   487→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   488→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   489→  const colName = m.left + 5;
   490→  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;
   491→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;
   492→
   493→  // Section header
   494→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   495→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   496→  textAt(doc, 'Teilnehmer nach Bundesland / Land', m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   497→  doc.y += 20;
   498→
   499→  // Column headers
   500→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   501→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   502→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   503→  textAt(doc, 'BUNDESLAND', colName, hy);
   504→  textAt(doc, 'TEILNEHMER', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   505→  textAt(doc, 'RAPPORTE', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   506→  doc.y += 16;
   507→
   508→  blStats.forEach((bl, i) => {
   509→    ensureSpace(doc, 12);
   510→    const rowY = doc.y;
   511→    if (i % 2 === 0) {
   512→      doc.save();
   513→      doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 12).fill(GRAY_50);
   514→      doc.restore();
   515→    }
   516→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   517→    textAt(doc, bl.bundesland, colName, rowY + 2);
   518→    textAt(doc, String(bl.participants), colPart, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   519→    textAt(doc, String(bl.reports), colRap, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   520→    doc.y = rowY + 13;
   521→  });
   522→
   523→  // Gesamt
   524→  ensureSpace(doc, 18);
   525→  const gy = doc.y;
   526→  doc.rect(m.left, gy, usable, 16).fill(NAVY);
   527→  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(WHITE);
   528→  textAt(doc, 'Gesamt', colName, gy + 4);
   529→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalParticipants), colPart, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   530→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalReports), colRap, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   531→  doc.y = gy + 20;
   532→}
   533→
   534→function renderParticipants(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, participants: Participant[]) {
   535→  if (participants.length === 0) return;
   536→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   537→
   538→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   539→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   540→
   541→  // Section header
   542→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   543→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   544→  textAt(doc, `Teilnehmer-Liste (${participants.length})`, m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   545→  doc.y += 20;
   546→
   547→  // Column positions
   548→  const colNum = m.left + 3;
   549→  const colCall = m.left + 25;
   550→  const colName = m.left + 90;
   551→  const colBz = m.left + usable - 135;
   552→  const colBl = m.left + usable - 90;
   553→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 35;
   554→
   555→  // Column headers
   556→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   557→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   558→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   559→  textAt(doc, '#', colNum, hy);
   560→  textAt(doc, 'RUFZEICHEN', colCall, hy);
   561→  textAt(doc, 'NAME', colName, hy);
   562→  textAt(doc, 'BEZ.', colBz, hy);
   563→  textAt(doc, 'BUNDESLAND', colBl, hy);
   564→  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 35, align: 'right' });
   565→  doc.y += 16;
   566→
   567→  participants.forEach((p, i) => {
   568→    ensureSpace(doc, 11);
   569→    const rowY = doc.y;
   570→    if (i % 2 === 0) {
   571→      doc.save();
   572→      doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 10).fill(GRAY_50);
   573→      doc.restore();
   574→    }
   575→    const ty = rowY + 2;
   576→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   577→    textAt(doc, String(i + 1), colNum, ty);
   578→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   579→    textAt(doc, p.callsign, colCall, ty);
   580→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_MED);
   581→    textAt(doc, p.name || '', colName, ty, { width: colBz - colName - 5 });
   582→    textAt(doc, p.bezirk_code || '', colBz, ty);
   583→    textAt(doc, p.bundesland_name || '', colBl, ty, { width: colRap - colBl - 5 });
   584→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   585→    textAt(doc, String(p.report_count), colRap, ty, { width: 35, align: 'right' });
   586→    doc.y = rowY + 11;
   587→  });
   588→  doc.y += 4;
   589→}
   590→
   591→function renderFooter(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument) {
   592→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   593→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   594→  ensureSpace(doc, 25);
   595→  doc.strokeColor(GRAY_200).lineWidth(0.5).moveTo(m.left, doc.y).lineTo(m.left + usable, doc.y).stroke();
   596→  doc.y += 8;
   597→  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   598→  textAt(doc, 'oeradio.at  ·  bosarsa.oeradio.at', m.left, doc.y, { width: usable, align: 'center' });
   599→}
   600→
   601→// ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────
   602→
   603→export function generateAggregatedPdf(
   604→  exercises: ExerciseSummary[],
   605→  stats: PdfStats,
   606→  from: string,
   607→  to: string,
   608→): PDFKit.PDFDocument {
   609→  const doc = new PDFDocument({
   610→    layout: 'landscape',
   611→    size: 'A4',
   612→    margins: { top: 30, bottom: 30, left: 40, right: 40 },
   613→    autoFirstPage: true,
   614→    bufferPages: true,
   615→  });
   616→
   617→  renderHeader(doc, 'Auswertung', `${formatDateDE(from)} – ${formatDateDE(to)}  ·  ${exercises.length} Übung${exercises.length !== 1 ? 'en' : ''}`);
   618→  renderSummaryCards(doc, stats, exercises.length);
   619→  renderPerRepeater(doc, stats.perRepeater);
   620→  renderExerciseTable(doc, exercises);
   621→
   622→  // Page 2: Austria map
   623→  if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   624→    doc.addPage();
   625→    renderAustriaMap(doc, stats.blStats);
   626→  }
   627→
   628→  // Page 3: Charts (always BL-level for readability, max 9 entries)
   629→  const chartData = stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));
   630→  if (chartData.length > 0) {
   631→    doc.addPage();
   632→    renderBarChart(doc, chartData);
   633→    renderPieChart(doc, chartData.map(d => ({ label: d.label, value: d.reports })));
   634→  }
   635→
   636→  // Page 4+: Bezirk/BL detail table
   637→  if (stats.bezirkStats.length > 0) {
   638→    doc.addPage();
   639→    renderBezirkTable(doc, stats.bezirkStats, stats);
   640→  } else if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   641→    doc.addPage();
   642→    renderBlTable(doc, stats.blStats, stats);
   643→  }
   644→
   645→  // Page 5+: Participants
   646→  doc.addPage();
   647→  renderParticipants(doc, stats.participants);
   648→  renderFooter(doc);
   649→
   650→  doc.end();
   651→  return doc;
   652→}
   653→
   654→export function generateExercisePdf(
   655→  exercise: { name: string; date: string; exercise_type?: string | null },
   656→  stats: PdfStats,
   657→): PDFKit.PDFDocument {
   658→  const doc = new PDFDocument({
   659→    layout: 'landscape',
   660→    size: 'A4',
   661→    margins: { top: 30, bottom: 30, left: 40, right: 40 },
   662→    autoFirstPage: true,
   663→    bufferPages: true,
   664→  });
   665→
   666→  const formattedDate = new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT', {
   667→    weekday: 'long', day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric',
   668→  });
   669→  renderHeader(doc, 'Auswertung', `${exercise.name} — ${formattedDate}`);
   670→  renderSummaryCards(doc, stats);
   671→  renderPerRepeater(doc, stats.perRepeater);
   672→
   673→  // Page 2: Austria map
   674→  if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   675→    doc.addPage();
   676→    renderAustriaMap(doc, stats.blStats);
   677→  }
   678→
   679→  // Page 3: Charts (always BL-level for readability, max 9 entries)
   680→  const chartData = stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));
   681→  if (chartData.length > 0) {
   682→    doc.addPage();
   683→    renderBarChart(doc, chartData);
   684→    renderPieChart(doc, chartData.map(d => ({ label: d.label, value: d.reports })));
   685→  }
   686→
   687→  // Page 4+: Bezirk/BL detail table
   688→  if (stats.bezirkStats.length > 0) {
   689→    doc.addPage();
   690→    renderBezirkTable(doc, stats.bezirkStats, stats);
   691→  } else if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   692→    doc.addPage();
   693→    renderBlTable(doc, stats.blStats, stats);
   694→  }
   695→
   696→  // Page 5+: Participants
   697→  doc.addPage();
   698→  renderParticipants(doc, stats.participants);
   699→  renderFooter(doc);
   700→
   701→  doc.end();
   702→  return doc;
   703→}
   704→

     1→import { Router } from 'express';
     2→import { getDb } from '../db/database.js';
     3→import { generateAggregatedPdf, generateExercisePdf } from '../services/pdf.js';
     4→
     5→export const pdfRouter = Router();
     6→
     7→// GET /api/v1/pdf/reports?from=...&to=...&types=...
     8→// Aggregated PDF across a date range
     9→pdfRouter.get('/reports', (req, res) => {
    10→  const { from, to, types } = req.query;
    11→  if (!from || !to) {
    12→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'from und to Parameter erforderlich (YYYY-MM-DD)' });
    13→    return;
    14→  }
    15→
    16→  const db = getDb();
    17→
    18→  let typeFilter = '';
    19→  const queryParams: any[] = [from, to];
    20→  if (types && typeof types === 'string' && types.trim()) {
    21→    const typeList = types.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    22→    if (typeList.length > 0) {
    23→      typeFilter = ` AND e.exercise_type IN (${typeList.map(() => '?').join(',')})`;
    24→      queryParams.push(...typeList);
    25→    }
    26→  }
    27→
    28→  const exercises = db.prepare(
    29→    `SELECT e.id, e.date, e.name, e.exercise_type,
    30→      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0) as participant_count,
    31→      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL) as report_count
    32→    FROM exercises e
    33→    WHERE e.date >= ? AND e.date <= ?${typeFilter}
    34→    ORDER BY e.date ASC`
    35→  ).all(...queryParams) as any[];
    36→
    37→  if (exercises.length === 0) {
    38→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Keine Übungen im gewählten Zeitraum' });
    39→    return;
    40→  }
    41→
    42→  const ids = exercises.map((e: any) => e.id);
    43→  const placeholders = ids.map(() => '?').join(',');
    44→
    45→  const totalParticipants = (db.prepare(
    46→    `SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND is_op_marker = 0`
    47→  ).get(...ids) as any)?.c || 0;
    48→
    49→  const totalReports = (db.prepare(
    50→    `SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL`
    51→  ).get(...ids) as any)?.c || 0;
    52→
    53→  const perRepeater = db.prepare(
    54→    `SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count
    55→    FROM signal_reports sr
    56→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    57→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0 AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL
    58→    GROUP BY sr.repeater_id
    59→    ORDER BY count DESC`
    60→  ).all(...ids) as any[];
    61→
    62→  const bezirkStats = db.prepare(
    63→    `SELECT bl.name as bundesland, bl.code as bundesland_code, bl.sort_order as bl_sort,
    64→      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
    65→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
    66→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
    67→    FROM signal_reports sr
    68→    JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code
    69→    JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code
    70→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    71→    GROUP BY bz.code
    72→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code`
    73→  ).all(...ids) as any[];
    74→
    75→  const blStats = db.prepare(
    76→    `SELECT
    77→      COALESCE(bl.name, 'Sonstige') as bundesland,
    78→      o.bundesland_code,
    79→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
    80→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
    81→    FROM signal_reports sr
    82→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    83→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    84→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    85→    GROUP BY o.bundesland_code
    86→    ORDER BY o.bundesland_code`
    87→  ).all(...ids) as any[];
    88→
    89→  const participants = db.prepare(
    90→    `SELECT o.callsign, o.name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
    91→      bl.name as bundesland_name,
    92→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count
    93→    FROM signal_reports sr
    94→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    95→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    96→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    97→    GROUP BY sr.operator_id
    98→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign`
    99→  ).all(...ids) as any[];
   100→
   101→  const stats = { totalParticipants, totalReports, perRepeater, bezirkStats, blStats, participants };
   102→
   103→  const pdfDoc = generateAggregatedPdf(exercises, stats, from as string, to as string);
   104→
   105→  const filename = `BOS-ARSA_Auswertung_${from}_${to}.pdf`;
   106→  res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/pdf');
   107→  res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename="${filename}"`);
   108→  pdfDoc.pipe(res);
   109→});

```
     1→# BOS-ARSA Log — Sitzungsbericht 24.02.2026
     2→
     3→## Erledigte Aufgaben
     4→
     5→### #1 — Rapport soll verschiebbar innerhalb der Bezirke sein
     6→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/1
     7→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
     8→- **Umsetzung:**
     9→  - **Bezirk-Dropdown im Bearbeitungsmodus:** Beim Klicken auf einen Rapport erscheint ein Bezirk-Auswahlfeld neben Rapport, Notizen usw. Bezirk wechseln und mit OK bestätigen.
    10→  - **Drag & Drop:** Rapport-Zeilen können per Drag & Drop zwischen Bezirk-Abschnitten verschoben werden. Das Ziel wird blau hervorgehoben. Funktioniert in Frequenzen- und OE-Link-Modus.
    11→  - Dropdown funktioniert auf allen Geräten (inkl. Mobilgeräte), Drag & Drop auf Desktop.
    12→  - Kein Backend-Änderung nötig — der PATCH-Endpunkt unterstützte `bezirk_code` bereits.
    13→
    14→### Datenbank-Backup
    15→- Produktions-Backup manuell über das bestehende Backup-Skript (`backup-bosarsalog.sh`) ausgelöst und auf Synology NAS verifiziert.
    16→- Zusätzliches lokales Backup in `backups/bosarsalog-backup-2026-02-24.db` (1,6 MB, Integritätsprüfung bestanden).
    17→
    18→### Repeater-Analyse
    19→- Detaillierte Analyse der gemeldeten Verwirrung bei Schöckl 2m/70cm und Lachtal im Setup durchgeführt.
    20→- **Ergebnis:** Kein Datenfehler — die Filterlogik (linked → OE-Link, non-linked → Frequenzen) funktioniert korrekt wie vorgesehen.
    21→
    22→### #2 — Option Bezirkskenner in Simplex Frequenzen anzuzeigen
    23→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/2
    24→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
    25→- **Umsetzung:**
    26→  - **Neuer Toggle pro Übung:** Admin-only Checkbox "Simplex Bezirke aktivieren" in der Übungs-Einrichtung (nach OE-Link-Toggle). Standardmäßig deaktiviert.
    27→  - **Schema-Migration:** Neue Spalte `simplex_bezirke INTEGER DEFAULT 0` in `exercises`-Tabelle (nicht-destruktiver ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN).
    28→  - **API:** GET/POST/PATCH Exercises-Routen unterstützen `simplex_bezirke`-Feld.
    29→  - **LandMode-Gruppierung:** Wenn aktiviert, zeigen Simplex-Frequenzkarten Bundesland-Unterüberschriften mit Bezirk-Zeilen (BezirkRow) statt flacher Liste (FlatReportRow).
    30→  - Bezirke werden aus den gewählten Bundesländern übernommen.
    31→  - **BL-Auswahl konfigurierbar:** Unterhalb des Simplex-Bezirke-Toggles erscheint ein kompaktes Bundesländer-Grid. Admin kann pro Übung auswählen, welche BLs in der Simplex-Gruppierung angezeigt werden. Beim erstmaligen Aktivieren werden alle 9 BLs vorausgewählt. Schema: neue Spalte `simplex_bl_codes TEXT` (komma-separiert, NULL = alle).
    32→  - Drag & Drop zwischen Bezirken funktioniert im gruppierten Modus.
    33→  - Nicht zugeordnete Rapporte erscheinen im "Sonstige"-Bereich.
    34→  - Toggle aus → Rapporte werden wieder flach angezeigt (Daten bleiben erhalten).
    35→  - **Geänderte Dateien:** `schema.ts`, `exercises.ts`, `ExerciseSetupPage.tsx`, `ExercisePage.tsx`, `LandMode.tsx`
    36→
    37→### #4 — Eingabe vom OP Rufzeichen wird nicht in Echtzeit übernommen
    38→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/4
    39→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
    40→- **Umsetzung:**
    41→  - **Neues WebSocket-Event `op_callsign_updated`:** Server empfängt das Event und broadcastet es an alle anderen Clients im selben Exercise-Room.
    42→  - **LandMode (Frequenzen-Modus):** Nach dem Speichern eines OP-Rufzeichens wird `op_callsign_updated` emittiert. Eingehende Events aktualisieren `opCallsigns`-State sofort.
    43→  - **BundMode (OE-Link-Modus):** Gleicher Pattern — nach API-Call wird emittiert. Eingehende Events mappen `repeater_id` zurück auf `blCode` via `blRepSelection` und aktualisieren `blOpCallsigns`.
    44→  - Cleanup: Socket-Listener werden beim Unmount/Re-Mount korrekt entfernt.
    45→  - **Geänderte Dateien:** `socket.ts` (Server), `LandMode.tsx`, `BundMode.tsx`
    46→
    47→---
    48→
    49→## Offene Issues
    50→
    51→### #3 — Bei den Repeatern bitte „eigener Repeater" dazufügen — Klärung
    52→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/3
    53→- Klärungsbedarf: benutzerdefinierte Repeater können bereits über Einrichtung hinzugefügt werden. Zu klären, ob weitere Funktionalität gewünscht.
    54→
    55→### #5 — Eventuell mehrere OPs bei Simplex
    56→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/5
    57→- **Status:** Geschlossen — bereits möglich
    58→- Mehrere OPs können kommagetrennt im OP-Feld eingetragen werden, z.B. `OE1OP1, OE2OP2`. Das Feld ist ein Freitext-Input ohne Beschränkung auf ein einzelnes Rufzeichen.
    59→
    60→### #8 — Download Auswertung komplett — Server-seitiges PDF
    61→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/8
    62→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
    63→- **Problem:** "Download Auswertung komplett" nutzte `html-to-image` (`toPng()`), das den gesamten DOM klonte — inkl. SVG-Karte, Chart.js-Diagramme, große Tabellen. Bei umfangreichen Auswertungen crashte der Browser (500MB+ RAM, Main Thread blockiert).
    64→- **Lösung:** PDF-Generierung auf dem Server mit PDFKit (pure JS, kein Chromium nötig).
    65→- **Umsetzung:**
    66→  - **Neue Datei `server/src/services/pdf.ts`:** PDF-Generator mit Render-Funktionen: Header (Navy-Leiste mit BOS-ARSA Branding), Summary-Cards, Per-Repeater-Grid, Übungstabelle, Austria-Karte (SVG-Pfade direkt via PDFKit gezeichnet mit Heatmap-Farben), Balkendiagramm (Rechtecke), Tortendiagramm (Kreissegmente), Bezirk/BL-Tabelle, Teilnehmer-Liste, Footer.
    67→  - **Neue Datei `server/src/routes/pdf.ts`:** Zwei Endpunkte: `GET /api/v1/pdf/reports?from=...&to=...&types=...` (aggregiert) und `GET /api/v1/pdf/exercises/:id` (Einzel-Übung).
    68→  - **Client:** `downloadFullPage()` in `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx` und `ReportsPage.tsx` ruft jetzt den Server-PDF-Endpunkt auf statt `toPng()`. Dateiname: `.pdf` statt `.png`.
    69→  - Einzelne Chart-Downloads (Karte, Balken, Torte) bleiben als PNG via `html-to-image` — **kein Funktionsverlust**.
    70→  - PDF: A4 Querformat, automatische Seitenumbrüche.
    71→- **Nachträgliche Fixes (4 Iterationen):**
    72→  - **Fix 1 — Tabellen-Layout:** `doc.text()` verschob Y-Cursor automatisch bei jeder Spalte → `textAt()`-Helper mit `lineBreak: false` und manueller Y-Steuerung pro Zeile.
    73→  - **Fix 2 — Austria-Karte schwarz:** PDFKit versteht kein CSS `rgb()` → Hex-Format (`#rrggbb`). `.fill().stroke()` Chaining funktioniert nicht → `fillAndStroke()`.
    74→  - **Fix 3 — Kartentext-Kontrast:** Labels bei dunklem Heatmap-Hintergrund (z.B. Kärnten mit vielen Teilnehmern) unsichtbar → `getLabelColor()` berechnet wahrgenommene Helligkeit und wechselt zu weißem Text bei dunklem Hintergrund.
    75→  - **Fix 4 — A4-Layout-Optimierung:**
    76→    - **Alle Repeater angezeigt:** `slice(0, 6)` Limit entfernt, Multi-Row Grid (6 pro Zeile, automatisch umbrechen).
    77→    - **Charts auf BL-Ebene:** Balken-/Tortendiagramm nutzt immer Bundesland-Daten (max 9 Einträge) statt Bezirke (40+) für Lesbarkeit. Bezirk-Detail bleibt in der Tabelle.
    78→    - **Charts auf eigener Seite:** Kein Split durch `ensureSpace` mitten im Chart-Block.
    79→    - **Pie-Legend Safety:** Berechnet verfügbaren Platz, zeigt „… +N weitere" bei Überlauf.
    80→  - **Offen:** Spaltenheader bei Seitenumbrüchen in Bezirk-/Teilnehmer-Tabellen wiederholen.
    81→- **Geänderte Dateien:** `server/src/services/pdf.ts` (NEU), `server/src/routes/pdf.ts` (NEU), `server/src/index.ts`, `server/package.json`, `client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`, `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`
    82→
    83→### #9 — Rapporte Verteilung schneidet Bundesland-Namen ab
    84→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/9
    85→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
    86→- **Problem:** Balken- und Tortendiagramm in der Webansicht (Auswertung) nutzten volle Bundesland-Namen (z.B. „Niederösterreich") als Labels — werden bei wenig Platz abgeschnitten.
    87→- **Fix:** Labels verwenden jetzt OE-Kurzform: `OE3` (Bundesland-Ebene) bzw. `OE3 AM` (Bezirk-Ebene mit Bezirkscode).
    88→- **Scope:** Nur Webansicht (Client). PDF-Labels waren bereits auf BL-Ebene.
    89→- **Geänderte Dateien:** `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`, `ReportsPage.tsx`
    90→
    91→### #6 — Rufzeichen doppelt eingeben führt zu Problemen
    92→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/6
    93→- **Status:** Erledigt
    94→- **Problem:** Wenn ein Rufzeichen doppelt eingegeben wird (gleicher Operator + gleicher Repeater), greift die UNIQUE-Constraint der DB und der Server gibt 409 zurück. Der Client versuchte den existierenden Report per `reports.find()` im lokalen State zu finden, um ihn zu PATCHen. Bei veraltetem State passierte **gar nichts** — kein Feedback, kein Update.
    95→- **Fix:** Server liefert bei 409 den existierenden Report als `existing_report` im Response-Body mit. Client nutzt diesen direkt statt im lokalen State zu suchen. `apiFetch` hängt den vollen Response-Body als `error.data` am Error-Objekt an.
    96→- **Geänderte Dateien:** `exercises.ts` (Server), `api.ts`, `LandMode.tsx`, `BundMode.tsx`
    97→

**Schema** (`server/src/db/schema.ts`):

**API** (`server/src/routes/exercises.ts`, `reports.ts`, `pdf.ts`):

**Reports display** (`ReportsPage.tsx`, `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`):
- Participant table shows callsign + suffixes

**PDF** (`server/src/services/pdf.ts`):
- Participant interface includes `suffixes` field
- `renderParticipants()` displays callsign + suffix

Build compiles cleanly. Ready for `./deploy-production.sh`.

```
Excellent! Now I have a complete understanding. Let me create a comprehensive summary document:

## Summary: BundMode Repeater Handling & "Eigener Repeater" Feature

Based on my exploration of the codebase, here's a detailed analysis:

### Current Architecture

**BundMode Component** (`/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/BundMode.tsx`):

1. **Linked Repeaters Loading (lines 42-83)**
   - Fetches all repeaters via `/api/v1/repeaters` and filters for `is_linked === true`
   - Stores in `linkedRepeaters` state
   - Initializes **per-Bundesland defaults** in `blRepSelection` dict:
     - Each Bundesland (BL) code maps to a repeater ID
     - Prefers a linked repeater within that BL, falls back to the first linked repeater overall
   - Loads existing `operator_callsign` from `exercise_repeaters` table via `/api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters`

2. **"Add Repeater" Functionality (lines 104-127, UI at 298-335)**
   - **UI:** `+ Umsetzer` button appears when `addRepBl` is set
   - **Form fields:** name, frequency (MHz), callsign
   - **Backend:** POST `/api/v1/repeaters` with:
     ```
     { short_name, site_name, frequency_mhz, callsign, band, type: 'repeater', bundesland_code }
     ```
   - **Result:** New repeater is created with `is_custom=1` (not marked as linked), added to `linkedRepeaters` state
   - These custom repeaters are treated as **regular (non-linked) repeaters** — they appear in Frequenzen setup, not OE-Link setup

3. **Repeater Dropdown Per BezirkRow (lines 531-542)**
   - Shows only **linked repeaters** in a `<select>`
   - Dropdown is **shared per BL** — all Bezirke in OE1 use the same selected repeater
   - Selection is managed via `repeaterId` state in BezirkRow, defaulting to `blRepSelection[bl.code]`
   - When user submits a report, the selected `repeaterId` is passed to `onSubmit`

4. **Exercise_Repeaters Management (lines 85-102)**
   - **POST** `/api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters` with `{ repeater_id, operator_callsign }`
   - Creates or updates the `exercise_repeaters` table row
   - WebSocket event `op_callsign_updated` broadcasts changes to all clients
   - The `operator_callsign` is stored per **linked repeater per exercise**

### Database Schema

**exercise_repeaters table** (`/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/schema.ts`, lines 78-83):
```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS exercise_repeaters (
  exercise_id TEXT REFERENCES exercises(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  repeater_id INTEGER REFERENCES repeaters(id),
  operator_callsign TEXT,
  PRIMARY KEY (exercise_id, repeater_id)
);
```

**Current columns:**
- `exercise_id` (FK) — which exercise
- `repeater_id` (FK) — which repeater
- `operator_callsign` — the operator's callsign for that repeater (text input)

**signal_reports table** uses:
- `repeater_id` — the repeater through which the report was given
- `einstiegspunkt_id` — optional entry point (for linked repeaters)
- No "operator's own repeater" field

### The Issue: "Eigener Repeater" (Own Repeater)

From the changelog (Issue #3 in `CHANGELOG-2026-02-24.md`, line 51-53):

**German:** "Bei den Repeatern bitte „eigener Repeater" dazufügen — Ziel ist des repeater des operators zu definieren."

**English:** "Please add 'own repeater' to the repeaters — the goal is to define the repeater (that the operator used / operator's own repeater)."

### What This Means in Context

In **BundMode (OE-Link)**, the current flow is:
1. Admin selects a **linked repeater per Bundesland** (the listening station)
2. Operators enter reports through that repeater
3. The report stores which **linked repeater** was used (`repeater_id`)
4. Admin can assign an **operator callsign** per linked repeater (`operator_callsign` in `exercise_repeaters`)

**The problem/feature request:**
- In reality, when multiple operators are on the same linked repeater (e.g., OE-Link), they may be **transmitting through different local repeaters** in their own Bundesland
- Example: 
  - OE1 operator listening on Rettenstein-Link (the "listening repeater")
  - OE8 operator listening on the same Rettenstein-Link
  - But OE1 operator transmits locally via "Hermannskogel 2m" (their own repeater)
  - And OE8 operator transmits via "Gerlitzen" (their own repeater)
- Currently, the system only tracks the **linked listening repeater**, not the **operator's own transmitting repeater**

### Current UI Elements Related to Repeaters in BundMode

1. **Per-BL Header (line 284):** Shows `blOpCallsigns` — operator callsign input
   - This is meant to be "the operator on this Bundesland"
   - Saved to `exercise_repeaters.operator_callsign`

2. **BezirkRow Form (lines 531-542):** Repeater dropdown
   - Filters only `linkedRepeaters` (where `is_linked=1`)
   - Used to select which linked repeater to log the report against
   - This becomes `repeater_id` in the signal_report

3. **Custom Repeater Creation (lines 298-335):** `+ Umsetzer` button
   - Currently creates a **non-linked repeater** (not marked as `is_linked`)
   - Doesn't show in BundMode repeater dropdown
   - Used only in Frequenzen (Land) mode

### What "Eigener Repeater" Feature Would Enable

The feature request is likely asking for:
- **Per-report "own repeater" field** — allow assigning which repeater the operator used for transmission, separate from the listening repeater
- This could be:
  - A dropdown next to the report form allowing operator to select their local repeater
  - Or a second repeater selection in the BezirkRow form: "Listening: Rettenstein-Link" + "Own repeater: Hermannskogel"
  - Or a per-report repeater override (e.g., in the edit form)

The feature would need:
- Schema: add `own_repeater_id` or similar to `signal_reports` (or `exercise_repeaters`)
- API: support this field in POST/PATCH reports
- UI: add repeater selector in BezirkRow or edit form to pick the operator's own repeater
- Possibly: allow creating/storing operator-specific repeater preferences

---

## Key Files to Modify (for implementation)

1. **`/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/schema.ts`** — Add column for own repeater
2. **`/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts`** — Add/update API endpoints to handle own repeater
3. **`/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/BundMode.tsx`** — Add UI to select own repeater
4. **`/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/pages/ExerciseSetupPage.tsx`** — Possibly add UI to configure operator repeater preferences

---

This exploration shows that BundMode is well-structured with per-Bundesland repeater selection and operator callsign tracking. The "eigener Repeater" feature would extend this to track which local repeater each operator used for transmission, not just which linked repeater they were heard on.

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the codebase. Let me create a detailed exploration report:

## Exploration Report: Operator Management and "Home Repeater" Implementation Strategy

Based on my exploration of the codebase, here's how operators are currently managed and how to add a "home repeater" field:

### 1. Current Operator Schema (Database)

**File**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/schema.ts` (lines 20-41)

The `operators` table has these stammdaten columns:
- `id` (INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)
- `callsign` (TEXT UNIQUE)
- `name`, `qth`, `gender`, `email`, `equipment`, `antenna`
- `bezirk_code`, `bundesland_code` (geographic references)
- `latitude`, `longitude` (coordinates)
- Flags: `is_bos_arsa`, `is_arena`, `is_portable`, `is_exercise_support`, `has_emergency_power`, `has_lighthouse_readiness`
- `created_at`, `updated_at`

**Key observation**: There is **NO** `home_repeater_id` field yet. This needs to be added as a migration.

---

### 2. Operator Create/Update Flow

**Backend Route**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/operators.ts`

**POST (line 57-80)**: Quick-add operator
- Accepts: `callsign`, `name`, `qth`, `bezirk_code`, `bundesland_code`
- Auto-derives bundesland from callsign prefix if not provided
- Auto-derives bezirk from QTH if not provided
- Does **NOT** currently support `home_repeater_id` on creation

**PATCH (line 101-133)**: Update operator
- Supports fields: `callsign`, `name`, `qth`, `bezirk_code`, `bundesland_code`, `is_bos_arsa`, `is_arena`, `is_portable`, `is_exercise_support`, `gender`, `email`, `equipment`, `antenna`, `has_emergency_power`, `has_lighthouse_readiness`, `latitude`, `longitude`
- Does **NOT** currently support updating `home_repeater_id`
- The implementation dynamically constructs SQL SET clauses (line 106-111), making it easy to add new fields

---

### 3. Frontend Operator UI

**File**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/pages/OperatorsPage.tsx`

This is a simple CRUD table with inline editing:
- **Add form** (lines 101-114): Creates operators with `callsign`, `name`, `qth`, `bezirk_code`, `bundesland_code`
- **Edit rows** (lines 132-144): Inline edit of all supported fields (currently includes `equipment` as an example)
- **Batch form**: Fields are managed in state via `newOp` object (line 11)
- **Delete button** (line 161-163)

Currently does **NOT** display or allow editing of `home_repeater_id`.

---

### 4. Operator Lookup/Autocomplete

**File**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/CallsignInput.tsx`

- Searches operators via `/api/v1/operators?q=...`
- Displays: `callsign`, `name`, `bezirk_code`, `bundesland_name`, `total_reports`
- When a suggestion is selected, calls `onSelect(operator)` callback
- Does **NOT** currently display or use `home_repeater_id`

**File**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/services/api.ts` (lines 1-16)

- `getOrCreateOperator()`: Gets operator by callsign or creates one if missing
- Used in `/LandMode.tsx` (line 133) when submitting reports

---

### 5. Signal Report Creation

**Backend**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts` (lines 242-298)

**POST `/exercises/{id}/reports`** accepts:
```typescript
{
  operator_id,
  repeater_id,
  readability,
  strength,
  db_over_s9,
  einstiegspunkt_id,
  is_op_marker,
  notes,
  bezirk_code,
  suffix
}
```

**Key observation**: The signal report is created with **explicit** `operator_id` and `repeater_id`. Currently, there is no "use operator's home repeater as default" logic.

**Frontend**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/LandMode.tsx`

- Lines 131-150: `handleBezirkSubmit()` creates a report
- Gets/creates operator via `getOrCreateOperator()`
- Parses rapport string (RST format)
- Sends to backend with `operator_id` and `repeater_id` from form

---

### 6. Repeaters Reference Data

**File**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/seed.ts` (lines ~150-276)

The seed data includes ~80 repeaters across all Austrian Bundesländer:
- Structured by Bundesland (OE1-OE9)
- Fields: `short_name`, `site_name`, `band`, `callsign`, `frequency_mhz`, `offset_mhz`, `ctcss_hz`, `burst_hz`, `type`, `is_linked`, `bundesland_code`
- Linked repeaters (OE-Link): Marked with `is_linked = 1` and have einstiegspunkte entries
- Custom repeaters supported: `is_custom = 1` flag

**Table structure** (`repeaters`):
- `id` (INTEGER PRIMARY KEY) — used as foreign key
- Standard repeater fields (frequency, CTCSS, etc.)
- `bundesland_code` TEXT (foreign key reference)

---

## Implementation Strategy for "Home Repeater" Field

### Step 1: Database Migration
Add column to `operators` table:
```sql
ALTER TABLE operators ADD COLUMN home_repeater_id INTEGER REFERENCES repeaters(id)
```

In `schema.ts`, add migration check similar to existing patterns (lines 219-225, 231-234).

### Step 2: Backend Routes (`operators.ts`)
- **Line 103**: Add `'home_repeater_id'` to the fields array in PATCH
- **Line 59**: Add `home_repeater_id` to POST body destructuring (optional on creation)
- No other logic changes needed—the dynamic SQL construction handles it

### Step 3: Frontend - Operators Table (`OperatorsPage.tsx`)
- **Line 11**: Add `home_repeater_id: null` to `newOp` state
- **Line 107**: Add new input/select for home repeater dropdown in add form
- **Line 138**: Add home repeater display/edit in table row
- **Line 139**: Add select dropdown in edit mode (needs to load repeaters list)

### Step 4: Frontend - Repeaters Dropdown Component
Create a reusable repeater selector component that:
- Fetches repeaters from `/api/v1/repeaters` or reference data
- Groups by Bundesland (like LandMode does)
- Shows `short_name` (e.g., "Magdalensberg linked") as primary label
- Shows band and frequency as secondary info

### Step 5: Frontend - Signal Report Form (Optional Enhancement)
If you want to auto-populate repeater when creating reports:
- In `LandMode.tsx`, after getting operator (line 133), check if operator has `home_repeater_id`
- Use it as a pre-selected default (optional, user can override)
- This improves UX for operators who typically use the same repeater

### Step 6: API Integration
- `GET /api/v1/operators/{id}`: Already returns all operator fields (line 35)
- `PATCH /api/v1/operators/{id}`: Add `home_repeater_id` support (automatic once added to fields array)
- No new endpoints needed

---

## Key Implementation Notes

1. **Foreign Key Constraint**: The repeaters table has `id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT`. The `home_repeater_id` will be a nullable integer reference (operators without a home repeater = NULL).

2. **Backwards Compatibility**: The field is nullable, so existing operators won't break. The PATCH endpoint already handles partial updates.

3. **Repeater Selection**: Since the schema allows custom repeaters (`is_custom` flag), the dropdown should include both OEVSV data and user-created repeaters.

4. **Display in Reports**: The signal_reports query (exercises.ts line 55-64) already joins operators. To show home repeater in reports, add a LEFT JOIN to repeaters on `o.home_repeater_id`.

5. **Relational Integrity**: Deleting a repeater won't cascade-delete operators (no ON DELETE CASCADE). You'd need to either:
   - Set `home_repeater_id = NULL` when a repeater is deleted
   - Prevent deletion of repeaters that have operators pointing to them
   - This is a business rule decision

---

## File Paths Summary

- **Schema**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/schema.ts`
- **Operators Route**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/operators.ts`
- **Operators Page UI**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/pages/OperatorsPage.tsx`
- **Callsign Input**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/CallsignInput.tsx`
- **LandMode**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/LandMode.tsx`
- **Exercises Route** (reports): `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts`
- **Repeater Seed Data**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/seed.ts`
- **API Service**: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/services/api.ts`

     1→import { Router } from 'express';
     2→import { getDb } from '../db/database.js';
     3→import { callsignToCountryCode } from '../utils/callsignCountry.js';
     4→import { qthToBezirkCode } from '../utils/qthBezirk.js';
     5→
     6→export const operatorsRouter = Router();
     7→
     8→// Search/list operators
     9→operatorsRouter.get('/', (req, res) => {
    10→  const db = getDb();
    11→  const q = req.query.q as string;
    12→  if (q) {
    13→    const pattern = `%${q.toUpperCase()}%`;
    14→    const operators = db.prepare(`
    15→      SELECT o.*, bl.name as bundesland_name,
    16→        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports sr WHERE sr.operator_id = o.id AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL) as total_reports
    17→      FROM operators o
    18→      LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    19→      WHERE o.callsign LIKE ? OR UPPER(o.name) LIKE ? OR UPPER(o.qth) LIKE ?
    20→      ORDER BY o.callsign LIMIT 50
    21→    `).all(pattern, pattern, pattern);
    22→    res.json(operators);
    23→  } else {
    24→    const limit = Math.min(Math.max(parseInt(req.query.limit as string) || 100, 1), 1000);
    25→    const offset = Math.max(parseInt(req.query.offset as string) || 0, 0);
    26→    const operators = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators ORDER BY callsign LIMIT ? OFFSET ?').all(limit, offset);
    27→    const total = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM operators').get() as any).c;
    28→    res.json({ operators, total });
    29→  }
    30→});
    31→
    32→// Get single operator with history
    33→operatorsRouter.get('/:id', (req, res) => {
    34→  const db = getDb();
    35→  const operator = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
    36→  if (!operator) {
    37→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Operator nicht gefunden' });
    38→    return;
    39→  }
    40→  const history = db.prepare(`
    41→    SELECT e.date, e.id as exercise_id,
    42→      GROUP_CONCAT(r.short_name || ': ' ||
    43→        CASE WHEN sr.is_op_marker = 1 THEN 'OP'
    44→        ELSE COALESCE(sr.readability || '/' || sr.strength || COALESCE(sr.db_over_s9, ''), '—')
    45→        END, ' | ') as reports
    46→    FROM signal_reports sr
    47→    JOIN exercises e ON e.id = sr.exercise_id
    48→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    49→    WHERE sr.operator_id = ?
    50→    GROUP BY e.id
    51→    ORDER BY e.date DESC
    52→  `).all(req.params.id);
    53→  res.json({ ...operator, history });
    54→});
    55→
    56→// Quick-add operator
    57→operatorsRouter.post('/', (req, res) => {
    58→  const db = getDb();
    59→  const { callsign, name, qth, bezirk_code, bundesland_code } = req.body;
    60→  if (!callsign) {
    61→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Rufzeichen erforderlich' });
    62→    return;
    63→  }
    64→  try {
    65→    const derivedBl = bundesland_code || callsignToCountryCode(callsign) || null;
    66→    const derivedBz = bezirk_code || (qth ? qthToBezirkCode(db, qth, derivedBl) : null);
    67→    const result = db.prepare(`
    68→      INSERT INTO operators (callsign, name, qth, bezirk_code, bundesland_code)
    69→      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    70→    `).run(callsign.toUpperCase(), name || null, qth || null, derivedBz, derivedBl);
    71→    const operator = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators WHERE id = ?').get(result.lastInsertRowid);
    72→    res.status(201).json(operator);
    73→  } catch (e: any) {
    74→    if (e.message?.includes('UNIQUE')) {
    75→      res.status(409).json({ error: 'Rufzeichen existiert bereits' });
    76→    } else {
    77→      throw e;
    78→    }
    79→  }
    80→});
    81→
    82→// Delete operator
    83→operatorsRouter.delete('/:id', (req, res) => {
    84→  const db = getDb();
    85→  const operator = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
    86→  if (!operator) {
    87→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Operator nicht gefunden' });
    88→    return;
    89→  }
    90→  const refs = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE operator_id = ?').get(req.params.id) as any).c;
    91→  if (refs > 0) {
    92→    res.status(409).json({ error: `Operator hat ${refs} Rapporte und kann nicht gelöscht werden` });
    93→    return;
    94→  }
    95→  db.prepare('DELETE FROM exercise_attendance WHERE operator_id = ?').run(req.params.id);
    96→  db.prepare('DELETE FROM operators WHERE id = ?').run(req.params.id);
    97→  res.json({ ok: true });
    98→});
    99→
   100→// Update operator
   101→operatorsRouter.patch('/:id', (req, res) => {
   102→  const db = getDb();
   103→  const fields = ['callsign', 'name', 'qth', 'bezirk_code', 'bundesland_code', 'is_bos_arsa', 'is_arena', 'is_portable', 'is_exercise_support', 'gender', 'email', 'equipment', 'antenna', 'has_emergency_power', 'has_lighthouse_readiness', 'latitude', 'longitude'];
   104→  const updates: string[] = [];
   105→  const params: any[] = [];
   106→  for (const f of fields) {
   107→    if (req.body[f] !== undefined) {
   108→      updates.push(`${f} = ?`);
   109→      params.push(f === 'callsign' ? req.body[f].toUpperCase() : req.body[f]);
   110→    }
   111→  }
   112→  // Auto-derive bezirk from QTH when QTH changes and bezirk_code not explicitly set
   113→  if (req.body.qth !== undefined && req.body.bezirk_code === undefined) {
   114→    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT bundesland_code, bezirk_code FROM operators WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id) as any;
   115→    if (existing && !existing.bezirk_code) {
   116→      const bl = req.body.bundesland_code ?? existing.bundesland_code;
   117→      const derived = qthToBezirkCode(db, req.body.qth, bl);
   118→      if (derived) {
   119→        updates.push('bezirk_code = ?');
   120→        params.push(derived);
   121→      }
   122→    }
   123→  }
   124→  if (updates.length === 0) {
   125→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Keine Änderungen' });
   126→    return;
   127→  }
   128→  updates.push("updated_at = datetime('now')");
   129→  params.push(req.params.id);
   130→  db.prepare(`UPDATE operators SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ?`).run(...params);
   131→  const operator = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
   132→  res.json(operator);
   133→});
   134→

   126→              <th className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-2"></th>
   127→            </tr>
   128→          </thead>
   129→          <tbody>
   130→            {operators.map(op => (

   135→                    <td className="px-3 py-1"><input value={editing.name || ''} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, name: e.target.value })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-24" /></td>
   136→                    <td className="px-3 py-1"><input value={editing.qth || ''} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, qth: e.target.value })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-24" /></td>
   137→                    <td className="px-3 py-1"><input value={editing.bezirk_code || ''} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, bezirk_code: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-12 uppercase" /></td>
   138→                    <td className="px-3 py-1"><input value={editing.bundesland_code || ''} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, bundesland_code: e.target.value })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-10" /></td>
   139→                    <td className="px-3 py-1"><input value={editing.equipment || ''} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, equipment: e.target.value })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-24" /></td>
   140→                    <td className="px-3 py-1 flex gap-1">
   141→                      <button onClick={() => handleSave(editing)} className="text-green-600 text-xs font-bold">OK</button>
   142→                      <button onClick={() => setEditing(null)} className="text-gray-400 text-xs">X</button>
   143→                    </td>
   144→                  </>
   145→                ) : (

   149→                    <td className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 text-gray-500 hidden sm:table-cell">{op.qth}</td>

   152→                        <span className="text-xs bg-gray-200 rounded px-1">{op.bezirk_code}</span>
   153→                      )}
   154→                    </td>

   156→                    <td className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-gray-400 hidden md:table-cell">{op.equipment}</td>
   157→                    <td className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 flex gap-2">
   158→                      <button onClick={() => setEditing({ ...op })} className="text-blue-700 hover:underline text-xs">
   159→                        Bearb.
   160→                      </button>
   161→                      <button onClick={() => handleDelete(op)} className="text-red-500 hover:underline text-xs">
   162→                        Löschen
   163→                      </button>
   164→                    </td>
   165→                  </>
   166→                )}
   167→              </tr>
   168→            ))}
   169→          </tbody>
   170→        </table>
   171→      </div>
   172→
   173→      {!search && (
   174→        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-4">
   175→          <button onClick={() => handlePage(-1)} disabled={page === 0} className="text-sm text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 disabled:opacity-30">

   177→          </button>
   178→          <span className="text-sm text-gray-400">Seite {page + 1}</span>
   179→          <button onClick={() => handlePage(1)} disabled={operators.length < 50} className="text-sm text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 disabled:opacity-30">
   180→            Weiter
   181→          </button>
   182→        </div>
   183→      )}
   184→    </div>
   185→  );
   186→}

{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/.claude/plans/synchronous-enchanting-island.md",
  "content": "# Issue #3: Eigener Repeater pro Operator (Stammdaten)\n\n## Context\nOperatoren haben oft einen \"eigenen\" Repeater, über den sie regelmäßig senden. Aktuell wird das nirgends erfasst. Das Ziel: optionales Freitext-Feld `home_repeater` in den Operator-Stammdaten, das in allen Auswertungs-Listen (Web + PDF) als zusätzliche Spalte angezeigt wird. Wenn leer → Spalte bleibt leer.\n\n## Entscheidung: Freitext statt FK\nEin Foreign Key auf `repeaters.id` wäre zu einschränkend — Operatoren könnten eigene Repeater haben, die nicht im System erfasst sind. **Freitext** (`TEXT`) ist flexibler und passt besser (z.B. \"Hermannskogel 2m\", \"Gerlitzen 70cm\", oder ein Rufzeichen wie \"OE8XVK\").\n\n## Änderungen\n\n### 1. Schema: `server/src/db/schema.ts`\n- Migration: `ALTER TABLE operators ADD COLUMN home_repeater TEXT` (wenn Spalte fehlt)\n- Nach den bestehenden Operator-Migrations-Blöcken einfügen\n\n### 2. API: `server/src/routes/operators.ts`\n- PATCH (Zeile 103): `'home_repeater'` zur `fields`-Liste hinzufügen — fertig, der dynamische SQL-Builder übernimmt den Rest\n\n### 3. API: Stats-Queries — `home_repeater` in Participants\nAlle 4 Participants-Queries brauchen `o.home_repeater`:\n- `server/src/routes/exercises.ts` → GET `/:id/stats` Participants-Query\n- `server/src/routes/reports.ts` → GET `/stats` Participants-Query\n- `server/src/routes/pdf.ts` → beide Participants-Queries (aggregiert + Einzel-Übung)\n\n### 4. Client Types: `client/src/types/stats.ts`\n- `Participant` Interface: `home_repeater: string | null` hinzufügen\n\n### 5. Auswertung Web: `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`\n- Teilnehmer-Tabelle: neue Spalte \"Repeater\" zwischen Bundesland und Rapporte\n- Zeigt `p.home_repeater || ''`\n\n### 6. Auswertung Web: `client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`\n- Gleiche Spalte wie ReportsPage\n\n### 7. Auswertung PDF: `server/src/services/pdf.ts`\n- `Participant` Interface: `home_repeater: string | null`\n- `renderParticipants()`: neue Spalte \"REP.\" zwischen Bundesland und Rapporte\n- Spalten-Positionen anpassen (colBl schmaler, colRep neu)\n\n### 8. Operator-Verwaltung: `client/src/pages/OperatorsPage.tsx`\n- Tabelle: neue Spalte \"Repeater\" (nach BL, vor Ausstattung)\n- Edit-Modus: Input-Feld für `home_repeater`\n- Add-Form: Input-Feld für `home_repeater`\n- `newOp` State: `home_repeater: ''` hinzufügen\n\n### 9. CHANGELOG: `CHANGELOG-2026-02-24.md`\n\n## Dateien\n\n| Datei | Änderung |\n|-------|----------|\n| `server/src/db/schema.ts` | Migration: ADD COLUMN `home_repeater` |\n| `server/src/routes/operators.ts` | PATCH fields: `home_repeater` |\n| `server/src/routes/exercises.ts` | Stats participants: `o.home_repeater` |\n| `server/src/routes/reports.ts` | Stats participants: `o.home_repeater` |\n| `server/src/routes/pdf.ts` | Beide participants queries: `o.home_repeater` |\n| `client/src/types/stats.ts` | `Participant.home_repeater` |\n| `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` | Spalte \"Repeater\" in Teilnehmer-Tabelle |\n| `client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx` | Spalte \"Repeater\" in Teilnehmer-Tabelle |\n| `server/src/services/pdf.ts` | Spalte \"REP.\" in PDF Teilnehmer |\n| `client/src/pages/OperatorsPage.tsx` | Spalte + Edit/Add für home_repeater |\n| `CHANGELOG-2026-02-24.md` | Dokumentation |\n\n## Verifikation\n1. `nix-shell -p nodejs --run \"npm run build\"` kompiliert sauber\n2. OperatorsPage: Feld editieren, Wert speichern, Seite neu laden → Wert da\n3. Auswertung (Einzel): Teilnehmer-Tabelle zeigt \"Repeater\"-Spalte\n4. Auswertung (Aggregiert): Teilnehmer-Tabelle zeigt \"Repeater\"-Spalte\n5. PDF-Download: Teilnehmer-Tabelle zeigt REP.-Spalte\n6. Operator ohne home_repeater: Spalte leer, kein Fehler\n7. Deploy mit `./deploy-production.sh`\n"
}

     1→# Issue #10: Suffix-Checkboxen (/m /p /am /mm) statt Freitext im Rufzeichen
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→Operatoren geben aktuell Suffixe wie `/m` (mobil), `/p` (portabel) direkt im Rufzeichen-Feld ein (z.B. "OE8YML/m"). Das ist fehleranfällig und uneinheitlich. Stattdessen sollen Checkboxen/Toggles für `/m`, `/p`, `/am`, `/mm` neben dem Rufzeichen-Feld erscheinen. Der Suffix wird separat gespeichert und in den Auswertungen mitangezeigt.
